# <span class="deva-large">भ</span> <span class="iast-large">bha</span>

<div class="dict-page-header">
  <span class="badge-count">508 entrées</span>
  <a href="../" class="back-link">← Index du dictionnaire</a>
</div>

---

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृ</span> <i>bhṛ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(bibharti)</i> ppr. <i>(bibhrat)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(bharati)</i> pr. md. <i>(bharate)</i> ppr. <i>(bharat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhṛta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhārya</i>, <i>bhṛtya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> soutenir, porter; supporter (au propre et au fig.); entretenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> porter sur soi, avoir — ca. <i>(bhārayati)</i> faire supporter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. φερω; lat. <i>fero</i>; ang. to bear; all. gebären; fr. -fère.<br> <i>rājyaṃ</i> <i>bhṛ</i> exercer la royauté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृष्ट</span> <i>bhṛṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bh_rjj" class="skt-link">bhṛjj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bh_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhṛṣṭā</i> frit; grillé, rôti.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rfga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृङ्ग</span> <i>bhṛṅga</i> <a href="#entry-bh_rfga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rfgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhṛṅgī</i> natu. zoo. abeille, bourdon; gros hyménoptère bleu foncé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> passereau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rfgaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृङ्गार</span> <i>bhṛṅgāra</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rfgaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rfgaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. vase d'or offert par <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> à <a href="#entry-Ubharatamuni" class="skt-link">Bharatamuni</a> et ses fils après leur première représentation théâtrale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. vase purificateur utilisé lors de la consécration d'un roi; il est composé des 8 alliages [<a href="#entry-a_s_tadhaatu" class="skt-link">aṣṭadhātu</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. vase utilisé par le directeur de théâtre [<a href="#entry-suutradhaara" class="skt-link">sūtradhāra</a>] lors du prologue [<a href="#entry-puurvarafga" class="skt-link">pūrvaraṅga</a>] d'une pièce pour purifier le <a href="#entry-jarjara" class="skt-link">jarjara</a> afin qu'il serve de bannière sacrée [<a href="#entry-indradhvaja" class="skt-link">indradhvaja</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rfgaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhṛṅgarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rfgaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. grande pie-grièche.<br> <a href="#entry-bh_rfga" class="skt-link">bhṛṅga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rfgin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृङ्गिन्</span> <i>bhṛṅgin</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_rfga" class="skt-link">bhṛṅga</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rfgin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-preta" class="skt-link">preta</a> Bhṛṅgī, adorateur de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> qui, ayant fait le vœu de n'adorer qu'un dieu, négligeait d'honorer <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>; ne pouvant circumambuler <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> seul, enlacé par celle-ci, il se transforma en insecte [<a href="#entry-bh_rfgii" class="skt-link">bhṛṅgī</a>] pour ce faire; <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> le maudit à être réduit à un squelette; ne pouvant plus se soutenir, il obtint une troisième jambe de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; pour l'obliger à honorer <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> s'unit à elle sous la forme androgyne <a href="#entry-Uardhanaariizvara" class="skt-link">Ardhanārīśvara</a>; on dit aussi qu'il représente le démon <a href="#entry-Uandhaka" class="skt-link">Andhaka</a> vidé de son sang; on le représente comme un squelette; il est aussi appelé Bhṛgiriṭi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rg">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृग्</span> <i>bhṛg</i> [onomatopée] ind. imite le craquement du feu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rgu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृगु</span> <i>bhṛgu</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_rg" class="skt-link">bhṛg</a><a href="#entry--u" class="skt-link">-u</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rgu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-mahar_si" class="skt-link">maharṣi</a> Bhṛgu «Crépitant», géniteur [<a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a>], né de la poitrine de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, ou bien né d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> au cours d'un <a href="#entry-brahmayaj_na" class="skt-link">brahmayajña</a> dirigé par <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>; il fut élevé par <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ucar_sa_nii" class="skt-link">Carṣaṇī</a>; son épouse était la belle et vertueuse <a href="#entry-Upulomaa_2" class="skt-link">Pulomā_2</a>; il est père de <a href="#entry-Uzukra" class="skt-link">Śukra</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ucyavana" class="skt-link">Cyavana</a>; au mariage de <a href="#entry-U_rciika" class="skt-link">Ṛcīka</a>, il enseigna à son épouse <a href="#entry-Usatyavatii_1" class="skt-link">Satyavatī_1</a> un rite pour permettre à elle et à sa mère d'avoir un fils; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] il officia au sacrifice de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, et <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> lui arracha la barbe; [SB.] comme il s'estimait supérieur en connaissances à son père <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>, celui-ci l'envoya dans l'au-delà; il y vit un homme coupant un autre avec sa hache, un homme mangeant un homme qui criait et un homme mangeant un homme silencieux; son père lui apprit qu'il étaient respectivement l'esprit d'un arbre, celui d'un animal, et celui d'une plante, se vengeant sur leur bourreau de ce qu'il leur avait fait subir en ce monde; [PP.] les sages [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>], ne pouvant décider quel dieu était le plus digne de vénération par les brâhmanes, lui demandèrent d'arbitrer; ne pouvant accéder à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> occupé maritalement, il le condamna à être adoré sous la forme du <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>; méprisé par <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> imbu de sa grandeur, il le condamna à ne pas être l'objet d'un culte; enfin il frappa du pied la poitrine de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> endormi pour le réveiller; celui-ci le traita néanmoins avec respect, gagnant ainsi son suffrage; <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a> avait un 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ œil sous son pied qui signifiait son ego orgueilleux; <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> le détruisit et lui montra la vanité de sa prétention à juger la <a href="#entry-trimuurti" class="skt-link">trimūrti</a>; <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a> est le patron des astrologues, et on lui attribue la <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgusa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">Bhṛgusaṃhitā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. myth. [RV.] les <i>bhṛgavas</i> sont une race d'êtres divins associés au feu, que <a href="#entry-Umaatarizvaa" class="skt-link">Mātariśvā</a> leur apporte; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhaargavaas" class="skt-link">Bhārgavās</a>.<br> <i>bhṛgor</i> <i>caru</i> myth. bouillie rituelle de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a>, absorbée par une épouse pour avoir un fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rgukaccha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhṛgukaccha</i> [<a href="#entry-kaccha" class="skt-link">kaccha</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rgukaccha" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np de la ville sacrée de Bhṛgukaccha «Rivage de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a>» sur la rivière <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a> (connue des grecs comme Barygaśa, mod. Broach/Bharuch au Gujarāt), où se trouve le temple de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgur_si" class="skt-link">Bhṛgurṣi</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubharukaccha" class="skt-link">Bharukaccha</a> — <a href="#entry-bh_rgukacchaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhṛgukacchā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rguprasrava_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhṛguprasravaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-prasrava_na" class="skt-link">prasravaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rguprasrava_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du Mont Bhṛguprasravaṇa «Cascade de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a>», dans l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>; <a href="#entry-Usagara" class="skt-link">Sagara</a> y fit pénitence 100 ans avec ses épouses pour obtenir des fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rgur_si">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhṛgurṣi</i> [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rgur_si" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhṛgurṣi, le <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rgusa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhṛgusaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rgusa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Bhṛgusaṃhitā, œuvre d'astrologie prédictive ancienne attribuée à <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bh_rgu" class="skt-link">bhṛgu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rguvallii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhṛguvallī</i> [<a href="#entry-vallii" class="skt-link">vallī</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rguvallii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ chapitre de la TU.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rjj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृज्ज्</span> <i>bhṛjj</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-bh_rg" class="skt-link">bhṛg</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(bhṛjjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> griller, rôtir; frire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rjjata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृज्जत</span> <i>bhṛjjata</i> [imp. <a href="#entry-bh_rjj" class="skt-link">bhṛjj</a>] ind. rôtissez!
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृत्</span> <i>bhṛt</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-bh_rt" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rt" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rt" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui porte, qui soutient; qui a, qui ait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृत</span> <i>bhṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bh_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhṛtā</i> porté, supporté, transporté; élevé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> acquis; payé, rémunéré — ifc. plein de — <a href="#entry-bh_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> salarié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rtaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhṛtaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rtaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> domestique, serviteur; salarié.<br> <a href="#entry-bh_rta" class="skt-link">bhṛta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rtha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृथ</span> <i>bhṛtha</i> <a href="#entry-bh_rtha" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. oblation (de <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृति</span> <i>bhṛti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-bh_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> entretien, fait d'élever ou de nourrir; salaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rtya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृत्य</span> <i>bhṛtya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bh_rtya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rtya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rtyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhṛtyā</i> devant être entretenu — <a href="#entry-bh_rtya" class="skt-link">m.</a> domestique, serviteur — ifc. qui élève ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bh_rza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भृश</span> <i>bhṛśa</i> a. <a href="#entry-bh_rza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bh_rzaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhṛśā</i> fort, puissant, violent; intense, excessif — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(bhṛśāyate)</i> devenir vigoureux; s'intensifier.<br> <i>bhṛśam</i> adv. fortement, puissamment, intensément; beaucoup, en grand nombre; fréquemment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> énergiquement, violemment; avec excès.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ</span> <i>bha</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_1" class="skt-link">bhā_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-bha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhā</i> semblable à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> suffixe de nom d'animal — nota. gram. mot fictif, variante morphologique intermédiaire qui a un comportement particulier en composition; opp. <a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-bhaa_2" class="skt-link">bhā_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_n">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भण्</span> <i>bhaṇ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(bhaṇati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhaṇita</i><i>)</i> parler, dire, adresser la parole à ⟨acc.⟩; appeler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_n_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भण्ड्</span> <i>bhaṇḍ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(bhaṇḍate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhaṇḍita</i><i>)</i> plaisanter; faire le bouffon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भण्ड</span> <i>bhaṇḍa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bha_n_d" class="skt-link">bhaṇḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> bouffon; mime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_n_din">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भण्डिन्</span> <i>bhaṇḍin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bha_n_d" class="skt-link">bhaṇḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_n_din" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. [<a href="#entry-Uhar_sacarita" class="skt-link">Harṣacarita</a>] np. de Bhaṇḍī, cousin aîné du roi <a href="#entry-Uhar_sa" class="skt-link">Harṣa</a> qu'il mit sur le trône à 16 ans à la mort de son frère <a href="#entry-Uraajya" class="skt-link">Rājya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_n_dita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भण्डित</span> <i>bhaṇḍita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bha_n_d" class="skt-link">bhaṇḍ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bha_n_dita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_n_dita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_n_ditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaṇḍitā</i> déridé, amusé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_n_taakii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भण्टाकी</span> <i>bhaṇṭākī</i> <a href="#entry-bha_n_taakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Solanum melongena</i>, aubergine; syn. <a href="#entry-vafgana" class="skt-link">vaṅgana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_nita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भणित</span> <i>bhaṇita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bha_n" class="skt-link">bhaṇ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bha_nita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_nita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_nitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaṇitā</i> dit, prononcé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à qui on a adressé la parole — <a href="#entry-bha_nita" class="skt-link">n.</a> parole <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. signature versifiée d'un poète à la fin d'un poème.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भष्</span> <i>bhaṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(bhaṣati)</i> aboyer, hurler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भट्ट</span> <i>bhaṭṭa</i> [pkt. <a href="#entry-bhart_r" class="skt-link">bhartṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. titre honorifique de Bhaṭṭa «Maître»; seigneur, prince; lettré, docteur, savant; cf. <a href="#entry-Uudbha_ta" class="skt-link">Udbhaṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukalla_ta" class="skt-link">Kallaṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukumaarila" class="skt-link">Kumārila</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukedaara_1" class="skt-link">Kedāra_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukau_n_da" class="skt-link">Kauṇḍa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ujayanta_1" class="skt-link">Jayanta_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaageza" class="skt-link">Nāgeśa</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaayaka" class="skt-link">Nāyaka</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na_3" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa_3</a>, <a href="#entry-Uniilaka_n_tha_4" class="skt-link">Nīlakaṇṭha_4</a>, <a href="#entry-Upraajya" class="skt-link">Prājya</a>, <a href="#entry-Umamma_ta" class="skt-link">Mammaṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-Umukula" class="skt-link">Mukula</a>, <a href="#entry-Uraamaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">Rāmakaṇṭha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzriidhara_1" class="skt-link">Śrīdhara_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> barde — a. <a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_t_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaṭṭā</i> vénérable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_taacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaṭṭācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_taacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. de Bhaṭṭācārya, titre prestigieux donné aux érudits; docteur, savant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_taara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भट्टार</span> <i>bhaṭṭāra</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_taara" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. de Bhaṭṭāra, titre d'«Honorable».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_taaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaṭṭāraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bha_t_taaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_t_taaraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_t_taarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaṭṭārikā</i> vénérable — <a href="#entry-bha_t_taaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> titre d'érudits, de princes ou de dieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. supérieur sédentaire vêtu assistant les renonçants [<a href="#entry-digambara" class="skt-link">digambara</a>] dans l'organisation des pélerinages, la consécration des images [<a href="#entry-prati_s_thaacaarya" class="skt-link">pratiṣṭhācārya</a>], l'administration des vœux et les rapports avec les laïcs — <a href="#entry-bha_t_taarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaṭṭārikā</i> reine-mère, régente; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvijayabha_t_taarikaa" class="skt-link">Vijayabhaṭṭārikā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. divinité tutélaire.<br> <a href="#entry-bha_t_taara" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_tamalla">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaṭṭamalla</i> [<a href="#entry-malla" class="skt-link">malla</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_tamalla" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du grammairien Bhaṭṭamalla, auteur du thésaurus de verbes <a href="#entry-Uaakhyaatacandrikaa" class="skt-link">Ākhyātacandrikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_tamata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaṭṭamata</i> [<a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">mata</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_tamata" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] école de <a href="#entry-Ukumaarila" class="skt-link">Kumārila</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_tanaaraaya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaṭṭanārāyaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-naaraaya_na" class="skt-link">nārāyaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_tanaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhaṭṭanārāyaṇa, écrivain du 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur du drame <a href="#entry-Uve_niisa_mhaara" class="skt-link">Veṇīsaṃhāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भट्टि</span> <i>bhaṭṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_ti" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du poète Bhaṭṭi; son poème [<a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-Uraava_navadha" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇavadha</a> est une version du <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a> destinée à enseigner la composition poétique et la correction grammaticale selon <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>; il vivait au 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle en Inde du Sud; il est qqf. identifié à <a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_tika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भट्टिक</span> <i>bhaṭṭika</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_tika" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhaṭṭika, fils de <a href="#entry-Ucitragupta" class="skt-link">Citragupta</a>; il est donné comme l'ancêtre de la lignée des scribes [<a href="#entry-kaayastha" class="skt-link">kāyastha</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_tikaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaṭṭikāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_tikaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Bhaṭṭikāvya, œuvre poétique de <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ti" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭi</a>; c'est le nom populaire du <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> <a href="#entry-Uraava_navadha" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇavadha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bha_t_ti" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_toji">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भट्टोजि</span> <i>bhaṭṭoji</i> <a href="#entry-bha_t_toji" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhaṭṭoji <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a>, grammairien de <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a> (~1550–~1630); il est not. l'auteur de la <a href="#entry-Usiddhaantakaumudii" class="skt-link">Siddhāntakaumudī</a> et de son commentaire <a href="#entry-Uprau_dhamanoramaa" class="skt-link">Prauḍhamanoramā</a>, et du <a href="#entry-Uzabdakaustubha" class="skt-link">Śabdakaustubha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_t_totpala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaṭṭotpala</i> [<a href="#entry-utpala" class="skt-link">utpala</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_t_totpala" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhaṭṭotpala ou <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> <a href="#entry-Uutpala" class="skt-link">Utpala</a>, astronome du 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Uhoraazaastra" class="skt-link">Horāśāstra</a>, et de la <a href="#entry-Ub_rhatsa_mhitaaviv_rti" class="skt-link">Bṛhatsaṃhitāvivṛti</a>, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Ub_rhatsa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">Bṛhatsaṃhitā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भट</span> <i>bhaṭa</i> [pkt. <a href="#entry-bh_rta" class="skt-link">bhṛta</a>] <a href="#entry-bha_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> mercenaire; serviteur, employé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. personne de caste mélangée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>āryabhaṭa</i>] hist. le mathématicien <a href="#entry-Uaaryabha_ta" class="skt-link">Āryabhaṭa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. soc. np. de la tribu hors-caste des <i>bhaṭās</i> ou <i>bhaṇḍās</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_tiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaṭīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bha_tiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_tiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_tiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaṭīyā</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Uaaryabha_ta" class="skt-link">Āryabhaṭa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bha_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाण्ड</span> <i>bhāṇḍa</i> <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> vase; récipient, pot, boîte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> outil, instrument, ustensile; ornement, trésor — <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. biens, marchandises, denrées — <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṇḍā</i> ifc. récipient de; testicule de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_n_daagaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṇḍāgāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aagaara" class="skt-link">āgāra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_daagaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> trésor; recueil.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_da" class="skt-link">bhāṇḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_n_daka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṇḍaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_daka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_daka" class="skt-link">n.</a> petit récipient, tasse, bol — ifc. marchandises, quantité de — <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_dikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṇḍikā</i> outil, instrument.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_n_dazaalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṇḍaśālā</i> [<a href="#entry-zaalaa" class="skt-link">śālā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_dazaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> entrepôt <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> portugais bancasal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_n_di">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाण्डि</span> <i>bhāṇḍi</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_n_da" class="skt-link">bhāṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_di" class="skt-link">f.</a> étui de rasoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_n_divaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṇḍivāha</i> [<a href="#entry-vaaha" class="skt-link">vāha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_divaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> barbier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_n_dizaalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṇḍiśālā</i> [<a href="#entry-zaalaa" class="skt-link">śālā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_dizaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> salon de coiffure.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaa_n_di" class="skt-link">bhāṇḍi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाण</span> <i>bhāṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bha_n" class="skt-link">bhaṇ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> récitation; monologue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. représentation théâtrale en un acte sous forme de monologue; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucaturbhaa_nii" class="skt-link">Caturbhāṇī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. récitation de la Loi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_naka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाणक</span> <i>bhāṇaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bha_n" class="skt-link">bhaṇ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_naka" class="skt-link">m.</a> récitant; hérault — <a href="#entry-bhaa_nikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṇikā</i> sorte de drame.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाष्</span> <i>bhāṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(bhāṣate)</i> pft. <i>(babhāṣe)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhāṣita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhāṣya</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(bhāṣṭum)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> parler, raconter, dire qqc. à qqn. ⟨2 acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाषण</span> <i>bhāṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhaa_s" class="skt-link">bhāṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> action de parler; bavardage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bonnes paroles, gentillesses <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. (théâtre) expression de satisfaction d'avoir atteint un but — <a href="#entry-bhaa_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṣaṇā</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाषा</span> <i>bhāṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhaa_s" class="skt-link">bhāṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> parole, langage parlé; langue ordinaire, vernaculaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_saacitraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣācitraka</i> [<a href="#entry-citraka" class="skt-link">citraka</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_saacitraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> jeu de mots; charade.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_saadvaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣādvaya</i> [<a href="#entry-dvaya" class="skt-link">dvaya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_saadvaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> paire de langues.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_saadvayottara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣādvayottara</i> [<a href="#entry-uttara_1" class="skt-link">uttara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_saadvayottara" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. figure de style poétique, où le mot formant la réponse est le même dans une langue vernaculaire.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaa_saadvaya" class="skt-link">bhāṣādvaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_saapaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣāpāda</i> [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_saapaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> plainte en justice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_saapariccheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣāpariccheda</i> [<a href="#entry-pariccheda" class="skt-link">pariccheda</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_saapariccheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Bhāṣāpariccheda «Jugement sur la langue», manuel de philosophie <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>-<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uvizvanaatha_3" class="skt-link">Viśvanātha_3</a>, aussi appelé la <a href="#entry-Ukaarikaavalii" class="skt-link">Kārikāvalī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_saasama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣāsama</i> [<a href="#entry-sama" class="skt-link">sama</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_saasama" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. énonciation pouvant être interprétée comme <a href="#entry-sa_msk_rta" class="skt-link">saṃskṛta</a> ou <a href="#entry-praak_rta" class="skt-link">prākṛta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaa_saa" class="skt-link">bhāṣā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_saav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣāvṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_saav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. np. de la Bhāṣāvṛtti «Glose (des règles grammaticales) de la langue», traité linguistique de <a href="#entry-Upuru_sottamadeva" class="skt-link">Puruṣottamadeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_sika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाषिक</span> <i>bhāṣika</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_saa" class="skt-link">bhāṣā</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaa_sika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṣikā</i> concernant la langue vulgaire — <a href="#entry-bhaa_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṣikā</i> langue; langage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_sikasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣikasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_sikasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. np. du Bhāṣikasūtra, recueil de règles d'accentuation du <a href="#entry-Uzatapatha" class="skt-link">Śatapatha</a> <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sika" class="skt-link">bhāṣika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाषिन्</span> <i>bhāṣin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaa_s" class="skt-link">bhāṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaa_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṣiṇī</i> qui parle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_sit_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाषितृ</span> <i>bhāṣitṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaa_s" class="skt-link">bhāṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaa_sit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sit_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṣitrī</i> locuteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाषित</span> <i>bhāṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhaa_s" class="skt-link">bhāṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaa_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṣitā</i> dit, parlé, énoncé — <a href="#entry-bhaa_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> langage; not. vernaculaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_sitapu_mska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣitapuṃska</i> [<a href="#entry-pu_mska" class="skt-link">puṃska</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_sitapu_mska" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. thème masculin d'un mot féminin utilisé en composition; cf. <a href="#entry-pu_mvadbhaava" class="skt-link">puṃvadbhāva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sita" class="skt-link">bhāṣita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाष्य</span> <i>bhāṣya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_s" class="skt-link">bhāṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> parole, discours <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> commentaire, annotation; glose, interprétation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. explication détaillée [<a href="#entry-vyaakhyaana" class="skt-link">vyākhyāna</a>] d'un <a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mahābhāṣya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_syapradiipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣyapradīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-pradiipa" class="skt-link">pradīpa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_syapradiipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Bhāṣyapradīpa «Lumière sur la glose», commentaire du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mahābhāṣya</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Ukaiya_ta" class="skt-link">Kaiyaṭa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uuddyotana" class="skt-link">Uddyotana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_syapradiipoddyota">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāṣyapradīpoddyota</i> [<a href="#entry-uddyota" class="skt-link">uddyota</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_syapradiipoddyota" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Bhāṣyapradīpoddyota «Révélation de la lumière sur la glose», commentaire par <a href="#entry-Unaagoji" class="skt-link">Nāgoji</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> du commentaire <a href="#entry-Upradiipa" class="skt-link">Pradīpa</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukaiya_ta" class="skt-link">Kaiyaṭa</a> du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mahābhāṣya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaa_syapradiipa" class="skt-link">bhāṣyapradīpa</a> <br> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाट्ट</span> <i>bhāṭṭa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaa_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_t_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_t_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāṭṭā</i> hist. concernant <a href="#entry-Ukumaarila" class="skt-link">Kumārila</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> — <a href="#entry-bhaa_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaka" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsaka</a> de son école <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. géo. le peuple des Bhaṭṭās — <a href="#entry-bhaa_t_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. l'œuvre de <a href="#entry-Ukumaarila" class="skt-link">Kumārila</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भा</span> <i>bhā_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(bhāti)</i> pft. <i>(babhau)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] <i>(abhāsīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhāta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> briller, resplendir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> paraître, se manifester, être en vue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (av. <a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>) faire pauvre figure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plaire à ⟨g.⟩ — ps. <i>(bhāyate)</i> (impers.) la splendeur irradie (de ⟨i.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. φαω; lat. <i>fāri</i>; fr. phare.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भा</span> <i>bhā_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaa_1" class="skt-link">bhā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> lumière, clarté; splendeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaadra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाद्र</span> <i>bhādra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">bhadra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaadra" class="skt-link">m.</a> abréviation du mois de <a href="#entry-bhaadrapada" class="skt-link">bhādrapada</a> — <a href="#entry-bhaadrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhādrī</i> sa pleine lune; cf. <a href="#entry-visarjana" class="skt-link">visarjana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaadrapada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhādrapada</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaadrapada" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. nom du mois de <a href="#entry-bhaadrapada" class="skt-link">bhādrapada</a> (août-septembre), mois des <a href="#entry-Ubhaadrapadaas" class="skt-link">Bhādrapadās</a> — <a href="#entry-bhaadrapadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhādrapadā</i> pl. astr. np. de Bhādrapadās «les pieds (du lit) de bon augure», astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] du Carré de Pégase, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-pro_s_thapadaa" class="skt-link">proṣṭhapadā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Upuurvabhaadrapadaas" class="skt-link">Pūrvabhādrapadās</a>, <a href="#entry-Uuttarabhaadrapadaas" class="skt-link">Uttarabhādrapadās</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaadra" class="skt-link">bhādra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaafgaasuri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाङ्गासुरि</span> <i>bhāṅgāsuri</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhafgaasura" class="skt-link">bhaṅgāsura</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaafgaasuri" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Bhāṅgāsuri, patr. de <a href="#entry-U_rtupar_na" class="skt-link">Ṛtuparṇa</a> «fils de <a href="#entry-Ubhafgaasura" class="skt-link">Bhaṅgāsura</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाग</span> <i>bhāga</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> portion, part, fraction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lot, destinée, sort; profit, bonne fortune.<br> <i>mahābhāgaḥ</i> Votre Éminence, Votre Excellence, Noble Sire [«qui a un grand destin»].<br> <i>yathā</i> <i>bhāgam</i> adv. conformément à son destin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagadheya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāgadheya</i> [<a href="#entry-dheya" class="skt-link">dheya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaagadheya" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. portion, part; propriété, biens; fortune, destinée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. part royale; taxe, impôt <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prospérité — <a href="#entry-bhaagadheya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. héritier, co-héritier — a. <a href="#entry-bhaagadheya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagadheya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagadheyii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāgadheyī</i> revenant comme part — <a href="#entry-bhaagadheyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāgadheyā</i> soc. taxe royale, impôt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagadugha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāgadugha</i> [<a href="#entry-dugha" class="skt-link">dugha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaagadugha" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. fonctionnaire répartiteur de dons ou de butin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagavata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भागवत</span> <i>bhāgavata</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhagavat" class="skt-link">bhagavat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaagavata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagavata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāgavatī</i> myth. relatif à <a href="#entry-Ubhagavaan" class="skt-link">Bhagavān</a>, à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; relatif à Dieu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagavatapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāgavatapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaagavatapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Bhāgavatapurāṇa «Geste du Seigneur», aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uzriimadbhaagavata" class="skt-link">Śrīmadbhāgavata</a>; c'est la vie légendaire de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, racontée par <a href="#entry-Uzuka" class="skt-link">Śuka</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a>, au roi <a href="#entry-Upariik_sit" class="skt-link">Parīkṣit</a>; ses 12 sections [<a href="#entry-skandha" class="skt-link">skandha</a>] sont une expansion des 6 tomes [<a href="#entry-a_mza" class="skt-link">aṃśa</a>] du <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nupuraa_na" class="skt-link">Viṣṇupurāṇa</a>; il fut composé en majeure partie entre le 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ et le 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècles en Inde du Sud; la tradition l'attribue à <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a>; il est conforme [<a href="#entry-saattvika" class="skt-link">sāttvika</a>] au canon <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>; son 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ chapitre (4000 vers sur un total de 18000) est particulièrement révéré.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaagavata" class="skt-link">bhāgavata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagazas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāgaśas</i> [<a href="#entry--zas" class="skt-link">-śas</a>] ind. par portions; chacun son tour; progressivement.<br> <i>bhāgaśas</i> <i>kalp</i> diviser en parts.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaaga" class="skt-link">bhāga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagiiratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भागीरथ</span> <i>bhāgīratha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhagiiratha" class="skt-link">bhagīratha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaagiiratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagiiratha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagiirathii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāgīrathī</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Ubhagiiratha" class="skt-link">Bhagīratha</a> — <a href="#entry-bhaagiirathii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāgīrathī</i> myth. np. de Bhāgīrathī, épith. de la <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a> «descendue sur Terre grâce à <a href="#entry-Ubhagiiratha" class="skt-link">Bhagīratha</a>» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. rivière himalayenne formant le haut cours du Gange, en amont de <a href="#entry-Uharidvaara" class="skt-link">Haridvāra</a>; elle prend sa source à <a href="#entry-Ugomukha" class="skt-link">Gomukha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भागिन्</span> <i>bhāgin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaagin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāginī</i> qui a droit au partage; qui possède une part; fortuné — ifc. qui partage; concerné par; qui hérite de — <a href="#entry-bhaagin" class="skt-link">m.</a> partenaire; propriétaire; héritier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le tout (opp. aux parties).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagineya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भागिनेय</span> <i>bhāgineya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhagin" class="skt-link">bhagin</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaagineya" class="skt-link">m.</a> neveu (fils d'une sœur); syn. <a href="#entry-svasriiya" class="skt-link">svasrīya</a> — <a href="#entry-bhaagineyii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāgineyī</i> nièce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagya_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाग्य</span> <i>bhāgya_1</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaaga" class="skt-link">bhāga</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaagya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagya_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagyaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāgyā_1</i> myth. relatif à <a href="#entry-Ubhaga" class="skt-link">Bhaga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui mérite sa part <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chanceux, fortuné — <a href="#entry-bhaagya_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> destinée; heureux destin, bonheur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. destin lié à son mérite [<a href="#entry-karma" class="skt-link">karma</a>] dans des existences antérieures — <a href="#entry-bhaagyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāgyā</i> ifc. chance de; destin de.<br> <i>bhāgyena</i> adv. par chance, heureusement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaagya_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाग्य</span> <i>bhāgya_2</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaagya_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagya_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaagyaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāgyā_2</i> à diviser; divisible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाज्</span> <i>bhāj</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-bhaaj" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaj" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaj" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a en partage, qui possède, qui jouit de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui habite, qui fréquente; qui adore.<br> <i>śokabhāk</i> affligé par le chagrin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाज</span> <i>bhāja</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhājā</i> ifc. qui prend part, qui participe à; relatif à; appartenant à — <a href="#entry-bhaaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> réceptacle, vase, vaisseau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> personne de grand mérite, de grande vertu ou intelligence — <a href="#entry-bhaajii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhājī</i> bouillie de riz.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaajana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाजन</span> <i>bhājana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaajana" class="skt-link">n.</a> vase, récipient, pot; réceptacle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (au fig.) là où un bien afflue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaajin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाजिन्</span> <i>bhājin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-bhaajin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaajin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaajinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhājinī</i> qui partage; qui participe à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाल</span> <i>bhāla</i> <a href="#entry-bhaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> front.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaama_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाम</span> <i>bhāma_1</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_1" class="skt-link">bhā_1</a><a href="#entry--ma" class="skt-link">-ma</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaama_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> lumière, splendeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaama_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाम</span> <i>bhāma_2</i> <a href="#entry-bhaama_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> colère, passion, rage — <a href="#entry-bhaamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāmā</i> femme passionnée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. cf. <a href="#entry-Usatyabhaamaa" class="skt-link">Satyabhāmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaamaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भामह</span> <i>bhāmaha</i> <a href="#entry-bhaamaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhāmaha, théoricien de poétique du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> (fin du 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Ukaavyaalafkaara" class="skt-link">Kāvyālaṃkāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaamat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भामत्</span> <i>bhāmat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-bhaa_1" class="skt-link">bhā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaamat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaamat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaamatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāmatī</i> brillant — <a href="#entry-bhaamatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāmatī</i> lit. np. de la Bhāmatī, «Brillante» exégèse du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a> de <a href="#entry-Uzafkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">Śaṅkarācārya</a>, due à <a href="#entry-Uvaacaspati_1" class="skt-link">Vācaspati_1</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a>; par ext., école phil. <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> qui en découle (opp. <a href="#entry-vivara_na" class="skt-link">vivaraṇa</a>); cf. <a href="#entry-Ukalpataru" class="skt-link">Kalpataru</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaamin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भामिन्</span> <i>bhāmin</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaama_2" class="skt-link">bhāma_2</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaamin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaamin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaminii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāminī</i> passionné — <a href="#entry-bhaaminii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāminī</i> femme passionnée; belle femme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Bhāminī «Belle», épouse de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha_1" class="skt-link">Jagannātha_1</a> <a href="#entry-Upa_n_ditaraaja" class="skt-link">Paṇḍitarāja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaminiivilaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāminīvilāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-vilaasa" class="skt-link">vilāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaminiivilaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Bhāminīvilāsa «Divertissement de <a href="#entry-Ubhaaminii" class="skt-link">Bhāminī</a>», poème en 384 stances de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha_1" class="skt-link">Jagannātha_1</a> <a href="#entry-Upa_n_ditaraaja" class="skt-link">Paṇḍitarāja</a> en mémoire à sa femme disparue prématurément.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaamin" class="skt-link">bhāmin</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaamita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भामित</span> <i>bhāmita</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaama_2" class="skt-link">bhāma_2</a><a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaamita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaamita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaamitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāmitā</i> véd. en colère; en rage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भान</span> <i>bhāna</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhaa_1" class="skt-link">bhā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> apparence, perception <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lustre, brillance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भानु</span> <i>bhānu</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_1" class="skt-link">bhā_1</a><a href="#entry--nu" class="skt-link">-nu</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> apparence; splendeur, brillance, éclat <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le Soleil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> roi, prince, seigneur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bhānu, Soleil, épith. de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a> et de divers dieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. titre honorifique — <a href="#entry-bhaanu" class="skt-link">f.</a> jolie épouse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaanucandra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhānucandra</i> [<a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaanucandra" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a> <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a> Bhānucandra (fin 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il était patronné par l'empereur moghol Akbar; son élève <a href="#entry-Usiddhicandra" class="skt-link">Siddhicandra</a> écrivit sa biographie <a href="#entry-Ubhaanucandracarita" class="skt-link">Bhānucandracarita</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaanucandracarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhānucandracarita</i> [<a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaanucandracarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Bhānucandracarita, biographie de <a href="#entry-Ubhaanucandra" class="skt-link">Bhānucandra</a> due à <a href="#entry-Usiddhicandra" class="skt-link">Siddhicandra</a>; son dernier chapitre est l'autobiographie de l'auteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaanudatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhānudatta</i> [<a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaanudatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhānudatta, brâhmane lettré du 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle originaire du <a href="#entry-Uvideha" class="skt-link">Videha</a>; il composa des œuvres d'esthétique de la poésie à la cour de <a href="#entry-Udevagiri" class="skt-link">Devagiri</a>-Daulatābād, not. la <a href="#entry-Urasama_njarii" class="skt-link">Rasamañjarī</a>, la <a href="#entry-Urasatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">Rasataraṅgiṇī</a>, et l'<a href="#entry-Ualafkaaratilaka" class="skt-link">Alaṅkāratilaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaanumat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhānumat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaanumat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaanumat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaanumatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhānumatī</i> brillant.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaanu" class="skt-link">bhānu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भार</span> <i>bhāra</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> charge, fardeau; corvée, labeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> poids, masse, grande quantité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> unité de poids, valant 20 <a href="#entry-tulaa" class="skt-link">tulā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. φορος; fr. -phore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaradvaaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भारद्वाज</span> <i>bhāradvāja</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bharadvaaja" class="skt-link">bharadvāja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaaradvaaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaradvaaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaradvaajii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāradvājī</i> (patr.) issu de <a href="#entry-Ubharadvaaja" class="skt-link">Bharadvāja</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. np. de lignée [<a href="#entry-gotra" class="skt-link">gotra</a>] Bhāradvāja; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugarga" class="skt-link">Garga</a>, <a href="#entry-Udro_na" class="skt-link">Droṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzaasa" class="skt-link">Śāsa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzunahotra" class="skt-link">Śunahotra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. soc. np. d'un clan de brâhmanes, d'une école védique; le 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">maṇḍala</a> du RV. est leur œuvre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaarahaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhārahāra</i> [<a href="#entry-haara_1" class="skt-link">hāra_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaarahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaarahaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaarahaara" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhārahāra</i> qui porte un fardeau — <a href="#entry-bhaarahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> porteur.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaara" class="skt-link">bhāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaarata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भारत</span> <i>bhārata</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bharata" class="skt-link">bharata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaarata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaarata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaratii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāratī</i> relatif à ou issu de <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a>; indien — <a href="#entry-bhaarata" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhG.] np. de Bhārata «Héros des <a href="#entry-bharataas" class="skt-link">bharatās</a>», adresse d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> par <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> le <a href="#entry-yaadava" class="skt-link">yādava</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>bhāratavarṣa</i>] cf. <a href="#entry-bharatavar_sa" class="skt-link">bharatavarṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. les Bhāratās, descendants de <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a> — <a href="#entry-bhaarata" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>bhāratākhyāna</i>] lit. np. du Bhārata_1, poème de 24000 vers, ancêtre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, issu du poème <a href="#entry-Ujaya_2" class="skt-link">Jaya_2</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>bhāramaṇḍala</i>] [«pays de <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a>»] l'Inde — f. cf. <a href="#entry-bhaaratii" class="skt-link">bhāratī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaarataratna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhārataratna</i> [<a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">ratna</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaarataratna" class="skt-link">n.</a> mod. plus haute distinction civile indienne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaratasamara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāratasamara</i> [<a href="#entry-samara" class="skt-link">samara</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaratasamara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. guerre entre les <a href="#entry-Ukauravaas" class="skt-link">Kauravās</a> et les <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍavās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaratavar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāratavarṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaratavar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Bhāratavarṣa, l'Inde; cf. <a href="#entry-bharatavar_sa" class="skt-link">bharatavarṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaratii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भारती</span> <i>bhāratī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-bhaarata" class="skt-link">bhārata</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaratii" class="skt-link">f.</a> éloquence; composition littéraire, art dramatique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. np. de l'ordre Bhāratī, l'un des 10 ordres de moines mendiants fondé par <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. commentaire du <a href="#entry-Unaa_tyazaastra" class="skt-link">Nāṭyaśāstra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaratiitiirtha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāratītīrtha</i> [<a href="#entry-tiirtha" class="skt-link">tīrtha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaratiitiirtha" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhāratītīrtha, <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> de <a href="#entry-Usaaya_na" class="skt-link">Sāyaṇa</a>; on lui doit l'<a href="#entry-Uadhikara_namaalaa" class="skt-link">Adhikaraṇamālā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaaratii" class="skt-link">bhāratī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaratiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāratīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaaratiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaratiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaratiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāratīyā</i> indien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaratiiyavidvatpari_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāratīyavidvatpariṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-vidvat" class="skt-link">vidvat</a>-<a href="#entry-pari_sad" class="skt-link">pariṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaratiiyavidvatpari_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> mod. association d'indologie indienne.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaaratiiya" class="skt-link">bhāratīya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-bhaarata" class="skt-link">bhārata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaravi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भारवि</span> <i>bhāravi</i> <a href="#entry-bhaaravi" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de Bhāravi, poète antérieur au 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur du <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> <a href="#entry-Ukiraataarjuniiya" class="skt-link">Kirātārjunīya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaargava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भार्गव</span> <i>bhārgava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bh_rgu" class="skt-link">bhṛgu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaargava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaargava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaargavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhārgavī</i> issu de, relatif à <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a> — <a href="#entry-bhaargava" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhārgava «De la race de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a>, de <a href="#entry-Ujamadagni" class="skt-link">Jamadagni</a>, de <a href="#entry-Uzukra" class="skt-link">Śukra</a>, etc. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. lignée des descendants de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a>; les Bhārgavās étaient les précepteurs [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] héréditaires des rois <a href="#entry-Uhaihayaas" class="skt-link">Haihayās</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uaurva" class="skt-link">Aurva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. nom de lignée [<a href="#entry-gotra" class="skt-link">gotra</a>] brâhmane <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] nom d'une arme donnée par <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a> à <a href="#entry-Ukar_na" class="skt-link">Karṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaargavaraaghaviiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhārgavarāghavīya</i> [<a href="#entry-raaghaviiya" class="skt-link">rāghavīya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaargavaraaghaviiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaargavaraaghaviiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaargavaraaghaviiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhārgavarāghavīyā</i> à propos de <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaargava" class="skt-link">bhārgava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भार्य</span> <i>bhārya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāryā</i> devant être entretenu ou nourri — <a href="#entry-bhaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> serviteur — <a href="#entry-bhaaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāryā</i> épouse, femme (aussi collectif au pl.).<br> <i>bhāryā</i> <i>bhū</i> se marier (pour une femme).<br> <i>gatavati</i> <i>vāyau</i> <i>dehāpāye</i> <i>bhāryā</i> <i>bibhyati</i> <i>tasmin</i> <i>kāye</i> [<a href="#entry-Ubhajagovindastotra" class="skt-link">Bhajagovindastotra</a>] Dès que le souffle a quitté ce corps, même l'épouse en est effrayée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaas_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भास्</span> <i>bhās_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-bhaa_1" class="skt-link">bhā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(bhāsati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhāsita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i><i>)</i> briller, resplendir; paraître — pr. md. <i>(bhāsate)</i> s'éclairer, devenir évident ou manifeste, être compris — ca. <i>(bhāsayati)</i> ca. md. <i>(bhāsayate)</i> faire briller, illuminer.<br> <i>yatra</i> <i>yat</i> <i>bhāsate</i> <i>tatra</i> <i>tat</i> <i>nāsti</i> Tout ce qui brille n'est pas or.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaas_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भास्</span> <i>bhās_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaas_1" class="skt-link">bhās_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaas_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> lumière, splendeur.<br> <i>tasya</i> <i>bhāsā</i> <i>sarvamidaṃ</i> <i>vibhāti</i> [ZU.] D'une telle lumière en vérité tout resplendit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भास</span> <i>bhāsa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaas_1" class="skt-link">bhās_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lumière, splendeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. impression mentale, évocation; imagination <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. zoo. vautour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Bhāsa, important dramaturge antérieur au 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on connaît une douzaine de ses pièces, dont <a href="#entry-Usvapnavaasavadattaa" class="skt-link">Svapnavāsavadattā</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucaarudatta_1" class="skt-link">Cārudatta_1</a>, et <a href="#entry-Ukar_nabhaara" class="skt-link">Karṇabhāra</a> — ifc. semblance de, illusion de; évocation de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaasarvaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāsarvajña</i> [<a href="#entry-sarvaj_na" class="skt-link">sarvajña</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaasarvaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhāsarvajña, philosophe du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> (9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Unyaayasaara" class="skt-link">Nyāyasāra</a> et du <a href="#entry-Unyaayabhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">Nyāyabhūṣaṇa</a>; on lui doit aussi un commentaire des <a href="#entry-Uga_nakaarikaas" class="skt-link">Gaṇakārikās</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaa_2" class="skt-link">bhā_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaskara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भास्कर</span> <i>bhāskara</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaas_1" class="skt-link">bhās_1</a>-<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaaskara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaskara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaaskaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāskarā</i> qui éclaire, brillant, lumineux — <a href="#entry-bhaaskara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soleil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bhāskara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a> «Qui illumine» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du philosophe Bhāskara_1 «Lumineux» (9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur du <a href="#entry-Ubhaaskarabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Bhāskarabhāṣya</a>, commentaire sur le <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a>; il professait la doctrine <a href="#entry-bhedaabhedavaada" class="skt-link">bhedābhedavāda</a>, rejetait la théorie de l'illusion [<a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">māyā</a>] et croyait en l'évolution [<a href="#entry-pari_naama" class="skt-link">pariṇāma</a>]; on lui doit aussi le commentaire <a href="#entry-Ubhagavadgiitaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Bhagavadgītābhāṣya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du logicien Bhāskara_2; cf. <a href="#entry-Ulaugaak_si" class="skt-link">Laugākṣi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cf. <a href="#entry-bhaaskaraacaarya" class="skt-link">bhāskarācārya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cf. <a href="#entry-bhaaskaraka_n_tha" class="skt-link">bhāskarakaṇṭha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaskaraacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāskarācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaskaraacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhāskarācārya_1, mathématicien du 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Bhāskarācārya_2, mathématicien et astronome du 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle (1114–1185); il dirigeait l'observatoire d'<a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>; il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Usiddhaantaziroma_ni" class="skt-link">Siddhāntaśiromaṇi</a> et de la <a href="#entry-Uliilaavatii" class="skt-link">Līlāvatī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaaskara" class="skt-link">bhāskara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaskarabhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāskarabhāṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaskarabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Bhāskarabhāṣya, glose du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a> due à <a href="#entry-Ubhaaskara_1" class="skt-link">Bhāskara_1</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uzaariirakamiimaa_msaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Śārīrakamīmāṃsābhāṣya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaskaraka_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāskarakaṇṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaskaraka_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe shivaïte du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> Bhāskarakaṇṭha (18⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur du Cittānubodhaśāstra et de la <a href="#entry-Umok_sopaaya_tiikaa" class="skt-link">Mokṣopāyaṭīkā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaaskararaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāskararāya</i> [<a href="#entry-raaya" class="skt-link">rāya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaaskararaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhāskararāya, philosophe du 18⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, spécialiste du culte de <a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>; il est l'auteur not. du <a href="#entry-Usetubandha" class="skt-link">Setubandha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaasura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भासुर</span> <i>bhāsura</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaasvara" class="skt-link">bhāsvara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaasura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaasura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaasuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāsurā</i> brillant, éclatant; splendide — ifc. excellent en, distingué par.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaasuratva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāsuratva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaasuratva" class="skt-link">n.</a> splendeur.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaasura" class="skt-link">bhāsura</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaasvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भास्वर</span> <i>bhāsvara</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaas_2" class="skt-link">bhās_2</a>-<a href="#entry-vara_2" class="skt-link">vara_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaasvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaasvara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaasvaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāsvarā</i> brillant, resplendissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bhāsvara «Resplendissant», un serviteur de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaasvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भास्वत्</span> <i>bhāsvat</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaas_2" class="skt-link">bhās_2</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaasvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaasvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaasvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāsvatī</i> brillant, resplendissant — <a href="#entry-bhaasvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> Soleil; éclat; lumière — <a href="#entry-bhaasvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāsvatī</i> myth. np. de Bhāsvatī «Splendide», capitale de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भात</span> <i>bhāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhaa_1" class="skt-link">bhā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhātā</i> brillant, resplendissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ayant apparu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाति</span> <i>bhāti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhaa_1" class="skt-link">bhā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaati" class="skt-link">f.</a> éclat, splendeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> évidence; perception, connaissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaav">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाव्</span> <i>bhāv</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(bhāvayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhāvita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhāvya</i><i>)</i> faire se manifester; produire, créer, causer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> encourager, réconforter; animer; promouvoir; montrer, manifester; considérer, reconnaître, savoir; honorer — dés. <i>(bibhāvayiṣati)</i> vouloir créer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
