    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाव</span> <i>bhāva</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> existence, présence; mode d'être; état, condition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> état d'esprit; disposition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> situation; devenir; transition vers, transformation en ⟨loc. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> caractère, nature, essence; réalité; vérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> comportement, conduite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> disposition psychologique, sentiment; pensée, intention <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> émotion, cœur; penchant, affection, amour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. l'existence et la copulation, 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ cause de souffrance [<a href="#entry-pratiityasamutpaada" class="skt-link">pratītyasamutpāda</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. procès lié à un verbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. aspect d'une planète <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Bhāva «Affectueux», qui personnalise l'an 8 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>] — ifc. état de (ex. <i>bālabhāva</i> jeunesse).<br> <i>x</i> <i>y</i> <i>bhāva</i> lien entre x et y.<br> <i>bhāvaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> éprouver de l'affection envers ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <i>bhāvaṃ</i> <i>dṛḍham</i> <i>kṛ</i> prendre une ferme résolution.<br> <i>bhāve</i> <i>prayoga</i> gram. voix impersonnelle (usage d'un vb. intransitif au passif).<br> <i>iti</i> <i>bhāvaḥ</i> lit. il en est ainsi (conclue une explication dans un commentaire).<br> <i>anyam</i> <i>bhāvam</i> <i>āpadyate</i> il meurt.<br> <i>bhāvo</i> <i>bhāvaṃ</i> <i>nigacchati</i> Qui se ressemble s'assemble.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavaabhinaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvābhinaya</i> [<a href="#entry-abhinaya" class="skt-link">abhinaya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavaabhinaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> gestuelle de l'émotion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavaabhivya_njana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvābhivyañjana</i> [<a href="#entry-abhivya_njana" class="skt-link">abhivyañjana</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavaabhivya_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> manifestation de l'émotion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavaanubandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvānubandha</i> [<a href="#entry-anubandha" class="skt-link">anubandha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavaanubandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> amour fidèle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. glose, signification synthétique d'une phrase; opp. <a href="#entry-zabdaartha" class="skt-link">śabdārtha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भावक</span> <i>bhāvaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaavaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvikā</i> qui cause, qui effectue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui imagine ⟨g. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a le goût artistique — <a href="#entry-bhaavaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> sentiment; affection.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavamizra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvamiśra</i> [<a href="#entry-mizra" class="skt-link">miśra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavamizra" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du médecin Bhāvamiśra (<a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a>, 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur du <a href="#entry-Ubhaavaprakaaza_1" class="skt-link">Bhāvaprakāśa_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भावन</span> <i>bhāvana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaavana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvanī</i> qui produit, qui fait apparaître; producteur, créateur — <a href="#entry-bhaavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> production, création — <a href="#entry-bhaavanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvanā</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. action exprimée par le verbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. création mentale, conception, imagination <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dévotion, foi (en ⟨loc.⟩).<br> <i>bhāvanam</i> <i>bandh</i> fixer l'imagination sur ⟨loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavaprakaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvaprakāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakaaza" class="skt-link">prakāśa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavaprakaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Bhāvaprakāśa, ouvrage de poétique en 10 chapitres dû à <a href="#entry-Uzaaradaatanaya" class="skt-link">Śāradātanaya</a>: il est aussi appelé Bhāvaprakāśana et Bhāvaprakāśikā <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. [<a href="#entry-Uaayurveda" class="skt-link">Āyurveda</a>] np. du Bhāvaprakāśa_1, traité encyclopédique d'<a href="#entry-aayurveda" class="skt-link">āyurveda</a> de <a href="#entry-Ubhaavamizra" class="skt-link">Bhāvamiśra</a> (1550).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavasvabhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvasvabhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-svabhaava" class="skt-link">svabhāva</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavasvabhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. nature de l'être.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavasvabhaavanityatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvasvabhāvanityatva</i> [<a href="#entry-nityatva" class="skt-link">nityatva</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavasvabhaavanityatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. inaltérabilité [«éternité de la nature de l'être»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavayat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भावयत्</span> <i>bhāvayat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-bhaav" class="skt-link">bhāv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaavayat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavayat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavayantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvayantī</i> devenant, qui se développe; produisant, créant.<br> <i>parasparaṃ</i> <i>bhāvayantaḥ</i> <i>śreyaḥ</i> <i>paramavāpsyatha</i> [BhG.] C'est en vous portant assistance mutuelle que vous atteindrez le but suprême.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भावि</span> <i>bhāvi</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-bhaavin" class="skt-link">bhāvin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाविक</span> <i>bhāvika</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaavika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvikā</i> réel, naturel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sensible; expressif — <a href="#entry-bhaavika" class="skt-link">n.</a> langage expressif ou passionné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. style de description du futur donnant l'illusion du présent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाविन्</span> <i>bhāvin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaavin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvinī</i> ifc. qui existe, qui devient, qui a l'habitude d'être <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> futur; imminent; prédestiné, inévitable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> comme il se doit, bien; capable; illustre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui manifeste, qui exhibe — <a href="#entry-bhaavinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvinī</i> belle femme; femme noble.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भावित</span> <i>bhāvita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhaav" class="skt-link">bhāv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaavita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvitā</i> créé, produit, obtenu, manifesté; transformé en ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bien disposé, réjoui, de bonne humeur; honoré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> occupé de, dévoué à, rempli de, absorbé par ⟨i. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvitā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> état de devenir; prédestination — ifc. fait de se conformer à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavitva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhāvitva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaavitva" class="skt-link">n.</a> état de devenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nécessité, inévitabilité.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaavi" class="skt-link">bhāvi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भावुक</span> <i>bhāvuka</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a><a href="#entry--uka" class="skt-link">-uka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaavuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvukā</i> en train de devenir; disposé à être — ifc. (après acc.) heureux de devenir — <a href="#entry-bhaavuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> bonheur, bien-être.<br> <i>āḍhyaṃbhāvuka</i> en train de devenir riche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भाव्य</span> <i>bhāvya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhaav" class="skt-link">bhāv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhāvyā</i> qui doit être effectué ou accompli — <a href="#entry-bhaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> il faut que ce ⟨i.⟩ soit — <a href="#entry-bhaavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> futur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भद्र</span> <i>bhadra</i> [<a href="#entry-bhand" class="skt-link">bhand</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhadraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhadrā</i> heureux, prospère, propice, fortuné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bon, plaisant, cher; vertueux, noble, pur, excellent; doué en ⟨loc.⟩ — voc. adr. mon bon ami, mon brave, mon cher; hé bien! <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Bhadra «Prospérité», <a href="#entry-dizaagaja" class="skt-link">diśāgaja</a> du Nord <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> type d'homme; cf. <a href="#entry-pa_ncamanu_syavibhaaga" class="skt-link">pañcamanuṣyavibhāga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> type d'éléphant; bœuf de trait — <a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">n.</a> bonheur, prospérité, fortune — <a href="#entry-bhadraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhadrā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Bhadrā qui coule à Mysore.<br> <i>bhadraṃ</i> <i>te</i> salut à toi! bonne chance!
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadraa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भद्रा</span> <i>bhadrā_1</i> [<a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">bhadra</a>] iic. (avec <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>) [«se faire beau»] se raser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadraakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhadrākaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_na" class="skt-link">karaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhadraakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> acte de se raser.<br> <a href="#entry-bhadraa_1" class="skt-link">bhadrā_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadraasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhadrāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-aasana" class="skt-link">āsana</a>] <a href="#entry-bhadraasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> trône <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] posture «bénéfique»: assis jambes écartées repliées, chevilles sous le scrotum, plantes des pieds jointes, mains tenant les pieds ou reposant sur les genoux.<br> <a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">bhadra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadraazva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhadrāśva</i> [<a href="#entry-azva" class="skt-link">aśva</a>] <a href="#entry-bhadraazva" class="skt-link">m.</a> [<i>bhadrāśvavarṣa</i>] myth. l'un des 9 bassins versants [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] du monde, au nord du mont <a href="#entry-Umeru" class="skt-link">Meru</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadrabaahu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhadrabāhu</i> [<a href="#entry-baahu" class="skt-link">bāhu</a>] <a href="#entry-bhadrabaahu" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhadrabāhu <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a>, né au <a href="#entry-Upu_n_dravardhana" class="skt-link">Puṇḍravardhana</a>, maître spirituel jaïne de <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta" class="skt-link">Candragupta</a> <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a>, auteur du <a href="#entry-Ukalpasuutra" class="skt-link">Kalpasūtra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भद्रक</span> <i>bhadraka</i> [<a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">bhadra</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhadraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhadraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhadrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhadrikā</i> bon, plaisant, brave; joli, fin; de belle prestance — <a href="#entry-bhadrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhadrikā</i> phil. [tantr.] np. de Bhadrikā «Belle», l'une des 8 puissances divines [<a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadrakaalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhadrakālī</i> [<a href="#entry-kaalii" class="skt-link">kālī</a>] <a href="#entry-bhadrakaalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Bhadrakālī «<a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a> l'Auspicieuse», forme de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> née de l'œil frontal de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; son temple à <a href="#entry-Usthaanezvara" class="skt-link">Sthāneśvara</a> est l'un des 51 <a href="#entry-zaktipii_tha" class="skt-link">śaktipīṭha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadramaat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhadramātṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-maat_r" class="skt-link">mātṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhadramaat_r" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Bhadramātā, fille de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukrodhavazaa" class="skt-link">Krodhavaśā</a>; elle engendra la lignée des éléphants par sa fille <a href="#entry-Uiraavatii_2" class="skt-link">Irāvatī_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadramukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhadramukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhadramukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhadramukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhadramukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhadramukhī</i> [«visage de bonheur»] terme de politesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhadramusta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhadramusta</i> [<a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">bhadra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhadramusta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhadramustaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhadramustā</i> natu. bot. <i>Cyperus rotundus</i>, souchet; cf. <a href="#entry-musta" class="skt-link">musta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भङ्ग</span> <i>bhaṅga</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bha_nj" class="skt-link">bhañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaṅgā</i> cassant; pétillant (<a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>) — <a href="#entry-bhafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> fracture, brisure, rupture; action de casser; division, interruption; ruine, destruction; refus, rejet; réfutation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inflexion, courbure, contraction, froncement; cf. <a href="#entry-tribhafga" class="skt-link">tribhaṅga</a>, <a href="#entry-samabhafga" class="skt-link">samabhaṅga</a> — <a href="#entry-bhafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaṅgā</i> bot. <i>Cannabis sativa</i>, chanvre indien, marijuana; boisson hallucinogène à base de chanvre broyé avec une meule et filtré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhafgaasura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaṅgāsura</i> [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] <a href="#entry-bhafgaasura" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Bhaṅgāsura, père de <a href="#entry-U_rtupar_na" class="skt-link">Ṛtuparṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhafga" class="skt-link">bhaṅga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhafgi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भङ्गि</span> <i>bhaṅgi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bha_nj" class="skt-link">bhañj</a>] <a href="#entry-bhafgi" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait de casser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tournant, courbe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manière détournée d'agir, circonlocution <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manière, comportement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manière de vêtir, toilette; mode <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apparence de, imitation de ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tromperie, fraude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ironie, répartie; humour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>bhaṅgī</i> id.<br> <i>bhaṅgyā</i> i. adv. d'une manière indirecte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhafgura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भङ्गुर</span> <i>bhaṅgura</i> [<a href="#entry-bhafga" class="skt-link">bhaṅga</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhafgura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhafgura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhafguraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaṅgurā</i> fragile, éphémère; variable; périssable; qui finit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भग</span> <i>bhaga</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> rétributeur; se dit not. de <a href="#entry-Usavitaa" class="skt-link">Savitā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chance, bonheur, fortune, prospérité; gloire; beauté; moralité; joie de la délivrance, énergie vitale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plaisir d'amour, volupté; vulve <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. véd. np. de Bhaga «Fortune», radieux [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>] mineur, adjoint de <a href="#entry-Umitra" class="skt-link">Mitra</a>, garant de l'attribution des biens et des maux, dieu de l'hymen; <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> [<a href="#entry-Ubhaganetrahara" class="skt-link">Bhaganetrahara</a>] lui creva les yeux; aveugle, il personnifie le Destin; astr. il préside le <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> <a href="#entry-Upuurvaphalgunii" class="skt-link">Pūrvaphalgunī</a> (δ du Lion) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bhaga_1 «Chance», l'un des 11 souffles divins [<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. division — <a href="#entry-bhaga" class="skt-link">n.</a> périnée masculin — <a href="#entry-bhagaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhagā</i> ifc. majesté, dignité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> slave bog.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagaafkura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagāṅkura</i> [<a href="#entry-afkura" class="skt-link">aṅkura</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagaafkura" class="skt-link">m.</a> clitoris.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भगाल</span> <i>bhagāla</i> <a href="#entry-bhagaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> crâne; syn. <a href="#entry-kapaala" class="skt-link">kapāla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagaalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagālin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagaalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhagālin, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «portant des crânes».<br> <a href="#entry-bhagaala" class="skt-link">bhagāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagaasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagāsya</i> [<a href="#entry-aasya" class="skt-link">āsya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagaasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui pratique la fellation»] homosexuel.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaga" class="skt-link">bhaga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagadatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagadatta</i> [<a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagadatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi Bhagadatta «Fortuné», qui régnait alors sur <a href="#entry-Upraagjyoti_sa" class="skt-link">Prāgjyotiṣa</a>; il s'allia aux <a href="#entry-kauravaas" class="skt-link">kauravās</a> dans la grande guerre; son éléphant de guerre <a href="#entry-Usupratiika_2" class="skt-link">Supratīka_2</a> était redoutable; il lança le missile [<a href="#entry-astra" class="skt-link">astra</a>] <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a> sur <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>, mais <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> s'interposa et le reçut comme une guirlande sur la poitrine; <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> le tua alors d'une flèche dans le cœur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagakaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagakāma</i> [<a href="#entry-kaama" class="skt-link">kāma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhagakaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhagakaama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhagakaamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhagakāmā</i> qui a un désir de rapports sexuels.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaganetrahara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaganetrahara</i> [<a href="#entry-netra" class="skt-link">netra</a>-<a href="#entry-hara" class="skt-link">hara</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaganetrahara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhaganetrahara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>-<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «qui a crevé les yeux de <a href="#entry-Ubhaga" class="skt-link">Bhaga</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagavadgiitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagavadgītā</i> [<a href="#entry-giitaa" class="skt-link">gītā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagavadgiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Bhagavadgītā «Chant du Seigneur», chant mystique du poème épique <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mahābhārata</a> où Dieu [<a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>] enseigne son devoir libérateur [<a href="#entry-karmayoga" class="skt-link">karmayoga</a>] à l'homme [<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagavadgiitaabhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagavadgītābhāṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagavadgiitaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Bhagavadgītābhāṣya, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Ubhagavadgiitaa" class="skt-link">Bhagavadgītā</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Ubhaaskara_1" class="skt-link">Bhāskara_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhagavadgiitaa" class="skt-link">bhagavadgītā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagavadgovinda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagavadgovinda</i> [<a href="#entry-govinda" class="skt-link">govinda</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagavadgovinda" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Bhagavadgovinda, poème de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Ubhajagovindastotra" class="skt-link">Bhajagovindastotra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhagavat" class="skt-link">bhagavat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भगवत्</span> <i>bhagavat</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaga" class="skt-link">bhaga</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhagavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhagavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhagavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhagavatī</i> fortuné, glorieux; bienheureux; vénérable, saint, divin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> Votre Révérence, Votre Honneur, Monseigneur; Mon Dieu — <a href="#entry-bhagavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhagavān, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> ou de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «le Bienheureux»; Dieu — <a href="#entry-bhagavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhagavatī</i> myth. np. de Bhagavatī «Bienheureuse», ou «Voluptueuse», forme tantrique de <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>; on lui consacre les cinq transgressions [<a href="#entry-makaarapa_ncaka" class="skt-link">makārapañcaka</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. np. de la Bhagavatī <a href="#entry-aaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">ārādhanā</a>, aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Umuulaaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">Mūlārādhanā</a>.<br> <i>bhagavos</i> véd. Ô Dieu (voc. archaïc).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagavatpadii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagavatpadī</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagavatpadii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la Bhagavatpadī «Issue du pied de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>», épith. de la source du Gange.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagavatpraapti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagavatprāpti</i> [<a href="#entry-praapti" class="skt-link">prāpti</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagavatpraapti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. la Réalisation Divine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भगी</span> <i>bhagī</i> [nom. <a href="#entry-bhagin" class="skt-link">bhagin</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagiiratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhagīratha</i> [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhagiiratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhagīratha «Chariot de gloire», roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Udiliipa_1" class="skt-link">Dilīpa_1</a>, petit-fils d'<a href="#entry-Ua_mzumaan" class="skt-link">Aṃśumān</a> de la lignée solaire [<a href="#entry-suuryava_mza" class="skt-link">sūryavaṃśa</a>]; il est l'exemple du roi très pieux; suivant les conseils de son directeur spirituel [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] le sage <a href="#entry-Utritula" class="skt-link">Tritula</a>, il suivit une pratique ascétique, et entreprit le sacrifice [<a href="#entry-yaj_na" class="skt-link">yajña</a>] d'<a href="#entry-agni_s_toma" class="skt-link">agniṣṭoma</a> pour donner l'ensemble de ses biens; il donna son royaume à son ennemi le roi voisin et devint un ascète errant, mendiant sa nourriture, dans des contrées lointaines où il était inconnu; il vint à passer par son ancien royaume, fut supplié de reprendre le trône, mais reprit son errance, en compagnie de <a href="#entry-Utritula" class="skt-link">Tritula</a>; le ministre d'un lointain royaume dont le roi était mort sans héritier le persuada d'y régner; plus tard, à la mort du roi lui ayant succédé, il revint régner sur son royaume à la demande de ses anciens sujets; par ses austérités et ses prières à <a href="#entry-Ugafgaadevii" class="skt-link">Gaṅgādevī</a> [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] il fit descendre [<a href="#entry-gafgaavatara" class="skt-link">gaṅgāvatara</a>] le Gange Céleste [<a href="#entry-Umandaakinii" class="skt-link">Mandākinī</a>] sur Terre par l'entremise de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Ugafgaadhara" class="skt-link">Gaṅgādhara</a> pour créer le Gange terrestre [<a href="#entry-Ubhaagiirathii" class="skt-link">Bhāgīrathī</a>] qu'il conduisit à l'Océan pour purifier les cendres des 60000 fils de son arrière grand-père <a href="#entry-Usagara" class="skt-link">Sagara</a> qui l'avaient creusé pour retrouver le cheval de son sacrifice; par son fils <a href="#entry-Uzrutanaabha" class="skt-link">Śrutanābha</a> (ou bien [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] <a href="#entry-Ukakutstha" class="skt-link">Kakutstha</a>) il est ancêtre de <a href="#entry-Uraghu" class="skt-link">Raghu</a> et donc de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhagii" class="skt-link">bhagī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भगिन्</span> <i>bhagin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhagin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhagin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaginī</i> fortuné, chanceux, glorieux — <a href="#entry-bhaginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaginī</i> sœur [«qui a la chance (d'avoir un frère)»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaginii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भगिनी</span> <i>bhaginī</i> iic. f. de <a href="#entry-bhagin" class="skt-link">bhagin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaginiipati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaginīpati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaginiipati" class="skt-link">m.</a> beau-frère (mari d'une sœur); syn. <a href="#entry-aavutta" class="skt-link">āvutta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaginii" class="skt-link">bhaginī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaginikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaginikā</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaginikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> petite sœur.<br> <a href="#entry-bhagin" class="skt-link">bhagin</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhagna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भग्न</span> <i>bhagna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bha_nj" class="skt-link">bhañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhagna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhagna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhagnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhagnā</i> détruit, cassé, brisé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a subi une fracture — <a href="#entry-bhagna" class="skt-link">n.</a> fracture de la jambe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhai_sajya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भैषज्य</span> <i>bhaiṣajya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhi_saj" class="skt-link">bhiṣaj</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhai_sajya" class="skt-link">n.</a> médecine, remède (contre ⟨g.⟩); efficacité curative.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaik_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भैक्ष</span> <i>bhaikṣa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhik_s" class="skt-link">bhikṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaik_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaik_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaik_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaikṣī</i> qui vit d'aumônes — <a href="#entry-bhaik_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> mendicité; nourriture mendiée.<br> <i>bhaikṣaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> demander l'aumône; mendier.<br> <i>bhaikṣaṃ</i> <i>car</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaik_suka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भैक्षुक</span> <i>bhaikṣuka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhik_suka" class="skt-link">bhikṣuka</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaik_suka" class="skt-link">n.</a> foule de mendiants — <a href="#entry-bhaik_suka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ stade de la vie brâhmanique, état d'ascète errant — <a href="#entry-bhaik_sukii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaikṣukī</i> écriture bd. ancienne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaik_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भैक्ष्य</span> <i>bhaikṣya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhik_s" class="skt-link">bhikṣ</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaik_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaik_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaik_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaikṣyā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-bhaik_sa" class="skt-link">bhaikṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhairava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भैरव</span> <i>bhairava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhiiru" class="skt-link">bhīru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhairava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhairava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhairavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhairavī</i> terrible, formidable, effrayant — <a href="#entry-bhairava" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhairava «le Terrifiant», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> sous sa forme farouche [<a href="#entry-ugra" class="skt-link">ugra</a>]; sa monture est un cadavre [<a href="#entry-zavaaruu_dha" class="skt-link">śavārūḍha</a>] ou un chien, et il porte la massue de crâne-humérus [<a href="#entry-kha_tvaafga" class="skt-link">khaṭvāṅga</a>] comme arme; il est souvent représenté ithyphallique [<a href="#entry-uurdhvalifga" class="skt-link">ūrdhvaliṅga</a>]; il est célébré [<a href="#entry-jayantii_2" class="skt-link">jayantī_2</a>] à <a href="#entry-bhairavaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">bhairavāṣṭamī</a>; il possède 8 temples à Bénarès [<a href="#entry-a_s_tabhairava" class="skt-link">aṣṭabhairava</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. <a href="#entry-yogin" class="skt-link">yogin</a> de la secte tantrique des adorateurs de <a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a> — <a href="#entry-bhairava" class="skt-link">n.</a> horreur, terreur — <a href="#entry-bhairavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhairavī</i> phil. [tantr.] np. de Bhairavī «la Terrible», l'une des 10 puissances [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] de la Force [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhairavaa_s_tamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhairavāṣṭamī</i> [<a href="#entry-a_s_tamii" class="skt-link">aṣṭamī</a>] <a href="#entry-bhairavaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. le 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine sombre du mois <a href="#entry-maargaziir_sa" class="skt-link">mārgaśīrṣa</a>; on y célèbre l'apparition de <a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a>, venu pour décapiter <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhairava" class="skt-link">bhairava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भज्</span> <i>bhaj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(bhajati)</i> pft. <i>(babhāja)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhakta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhajya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> diviser, partager, distribuer; allouer ⟨acc.⟩ à ⟨dat. g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pratiquer; not. adorer, prier — pr. md. <i>(bhajate)</i> recevoir en partage, obtenir, recouvrer, posséder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> servir, révérer, adorer, aimer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se rendre dans, sur; suivre (un chemin) — dés. md. <i>(bhikṣate)</i> cf. <a href="#entry-bhik_s" class="skt-link">bhikṣ</a>.<br> <i>āsanaṃ</i> <i>bhaj</i> s'asseoir.<br> <i>nidrāṃ</i> <i>bhaj</i> s'endormir.<br> <i>mūrchāṃ</i> <i>bhaj</i> s'évanouir.<br> <i>maunaṃ</i> <i>bhaj</i> se taire.<br> <i>punar</i> <i>bhaj</i> revenir à.<br> <i>etan</i> <i>māṃ</i> <i>bhajati</i> ceci me revient.<br> <i>ajasraṃ</i> <i>bhaje</i> je prie sans cesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhajagovinda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भजगोविन्द</span> <i>bhajagovinda</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-bhagavadgovinda" class="skt-link">bhagavadgovinda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhajagovindastotra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhajagovindastotra</i> [<a href="#entry-stotra" class="skt-link">stotra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhajagovindastotra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Bhajagovindastotra, poème philosophique de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a> en 33 stances; il exprime l'impermanence de toutes choses et prône le renoncement; cf. <a href="#entry-Umohamudgara" class="skt-link">Mohamudgara</a>.<br> <i>saṃprāpte</i> <i>sannihite</i> <i>kāle</i> <i>nahi</i> <i>nahi</i> <i>rakṣati</i> <i>ḍuṅkṛñkaraṇe</i> [<a href="#entry-Ubhajagovindastotra" class="skt-link">Bhajagovindastotra</a>] Lorsque ton heure sera venue, l'érudition grammaticale ne te sera d'aucun secours.<br> <a href="#entry-bhajagovinda" class="skt-link">bhajagovinda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhajaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भजक</span> <i>bhajaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhajaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhajaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhajikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhajikā</i> distributeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adorateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhajana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भजन</span> <i>bhajana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] <a href="#entry-bhajana" class="skt-link">n.</a> partage, distribution <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adoration, culte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. assemblée de chant dévotionnel [<a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhajaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भजनीय</span> <i>bhajanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhajaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhajaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhajaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhajanīyā</i> que l'on doit honorer; vénérable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhajya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भज्य</span> <i>bhajya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhajya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhajya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhajyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhajyā</i> que l'on doit honorer; vénérable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhak_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्ष्</span> <i>bhakṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(bhakṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhakṣita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhakṣya</i>, <i>bhakṣaṇīya</i><i>)</i> manger, consommer; boire, absorber; dévorer, engloutir; épuiser, détruire — ca. <i>(bhakṣayati)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>bhakṣayitvā</i><i>)</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्ष</span> <i>bhakṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhak_s" class="skt-link">bhakṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de manger ou de boire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nourriture, boisson, victuailles — ifc. fait de se nourrir de ⟨iic.⟩ — ifc. qui se nourrit de ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhak_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्षण</span> <i>bhakṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhak_s" class="skt-link">bhakṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhak_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de manger, de se nourrir; repas, restauration.<br> <i>bhakṣaṇaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> manger, consommer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhak_sa_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्षणीय</span> <i>bhakṣaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-bhak_s" class="skt-link">bhakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhak_sa_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_sa_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_sa_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhakṣaṇīyā</i> qui peut être mangé, comestible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhak_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्षक</span> <i>bhakṣaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhak_s" class="skt-link">bhakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhak_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhakṣikā</i> mangeur; vorace, gourmand, glouton — ifc. mangeur de ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-bhak_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> nourriture; repas — <a href="#entry-bhak_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhakṣikā</i> fait de manger; mastication.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhak_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्षिन्</span> <i>bhakṣin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhak_s" class="skt-link">bhakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhak_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhakṣiṇī</i> qui mange, qui dévore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhak_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्षित</span> <i>bhakṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhak_s" class="skt-link">bhakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhak_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhakṣitā</i> mangé, consommé, dévoré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mal prononcé, marmonné (mot) — <a href="#entry-bhak_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'avoir été mangé par ⟨i.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhak_sitavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्षितवत्</span> <i>bhakṣitavat</i> [ppa. <a href="#entry-bhak_s" class="skt-link">bhakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhak_sitavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_sitavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_sitavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhakṣitavatī</i> qui a mangé.<br> <i>nāham</i> <i>bhakṣitavānambe</i> <i>ityuktavāñchrīkṛṣṇaḥ</i> <i>bhāgavate</i> «Je n'(en) ai pas mangé Maman» dit <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a> <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> dans le <a href="#entry-Ubhaagavatapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Bhāgavatapurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhak_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्ष्य</span> <i>bhakṣya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhak_s" class="skt-link">bhakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhak_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhak_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhakṣyā</i> bon à manger, comestible — <a href="#entry-bhak_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> aliment, nourriture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भकार</span> <i>bhakāra</i> [<i>(bha)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>bha</i>’.<br> <i>bhakāraḥ</i> <i>kumbhakarṇe'sti</i> <i>bhakāro'sti</i> <i>vibhīṣaṇe</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>tasmāt</i> <i>rākṣasarājo'yam</i> <i>rābhaṇo</i> <i>na</i> <i>tu</i> <i>rāvaṇaḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> Il y a le son ‘<i>bha</i>’ dans <a href="#entry-Ukumbhakar_na" class="skt-link">Kumbhakarṇa</a>, le son ‘<i>bha</i>’ est dans <a href="#entry-Uvibhii_sa_na" class="skt-link">Vibhīṣaṇa</a>; ainsi le roi des vampires doit être Rābhaṇa plutôt que <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> (scolastique absurde d'un <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a> dans une légende sur le jeune <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhakta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्त</span> <i>bhakta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaktā</i> dévoué à ⟨g.⟩, fidèle, aimant; dévot, pieux adepte de — <a href="#entry-bhakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> adepte, pieux adorateur — <a href="#entry-bhakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> nourriture, riz (cuit).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaktapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaktapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaktapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Bhaktapura, ancienne capitale du Népal, fondée au 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle par le roi Devamalla, à 13 km à l'est de <a href="#entry-Ukathmandu" class="skt-link">Kathmandu</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhakta" class="skt-link">bhakta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhakti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भक्ति</span> <i>bhakti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] <a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">f.</a> amour, zèle, dévotion, ferveur, adoration; fidélité, hommage; extase <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. culte, adoration; not. culte extatique de l'amour divin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ordre, série, séquence — ifc. cas de, part de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaktimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaktimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaktimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaktimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaktimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaktimatī</i> dévoué, attaché, fidèle à ⟨loc. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dévot; adepte du culte de l'amour divin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaktisiddhaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaktisiddhānta</i> [<a href="#entry-siddhaanta" class="skt-link">siddhānta</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaktisiddhaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. np. de Bhaktisiddhānta, titre de lettrés et d'ouvrages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaktisuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaktisūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaktisuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Bhaktisūtra, recueil d'aphorismes dévotionnels attribué à <a href="#entry-Uzaa_n_dilya" class="skt-link">Śāṇḍilya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaktivedaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaktivedānta</i> [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaktivedaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. np. de Bhaktivedānta, titre de lettré de la tradition <a href="#entry-gau_diiya" class="skt-link">gauḍīya</a> <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uprabhupaada" class="skt-link">Prabhupāda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaktiyoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaktiyoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaktiyoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. réalisation spirituelle par la voie de la dévotion; cf. <a href="#entry-saamiipya" class="skt-link">sāmīpya</a>, <a href="#entry-saalokya" class="skt-link">sālokya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhalla">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भल्ल</span> <i>bhalla</i> <a href="#entry-bhalla" class="skt-link">m.</a> sorte de flèche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. ours.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhallastambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhallastambha</i> [<a href="#entry-stambha" class="skt-link">stambha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhallastambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> poteau d'exercice physique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhalluuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhallūka</i> <a href="#entry-bhalluuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. ours.<br> <a href="#entry-bhalla" class="skt-link">bhalla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhand">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भन्द्</span> <i>bhand</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(bhandate)</i> être loué; prospérer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être propice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भर</span> <i>bhara</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bharā</i> ifc. qui porte, qui soutient; qui supporte, qui maintient — <a href="#entry-bhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> charge, fardeau, poids, masse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> unité de poids; syn. <a href="#entry-bhaara" class="skt-link">bhāra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grande quantité, multitude — ifc. excès de.<br> <i>bhareṇa</i> var. <i>bharāt</i> adv. en masse, au maximum, à fond.<br> <i>bharaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> assumer une charge, prendre un fardeau; au fig. faire son possible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भरण</span> <i>bharaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> soutien, fait de nourrir; fait de soutenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> frais d'entretien, salaire — <a href="#entry-bhara_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bharaṇī</i> astr. np. de Bharaṇī «Celle qui enlève», 28⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à la Mouche; <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> y préside.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhara_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भरट</span> <i>bharaṭa</i> <a href="#entry-bhara_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> potier; serviteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhara_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharaṭaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-bhara_taka" class="skt-link">m.</a> mendiant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhara_takadvaatri_mzikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharaṭakadvātriṃśikā</i> [<a href="#entry-dvaatri_mzikaa" class="skt-link">dvātriṃśikā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhara_takadvaatri_mzikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Bharaṭakadvātriṃśikā, collection <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a> de 32 contes, satire des ascètes <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhara_taka" class="skt-link">bharaṭaka</a> <br> <a href="#entry-bhara_ta" class="skt-link">bharaṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भरद्</span> <i>bharad</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-bharat" class="skt-link">bharat</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharadvaaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharadvāja</i> [<a href="#entry-vaaja" class="skt-link">vāja</a>] <a href="#entry-bharadvaaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> alouette <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. véd. np. du patriarche [<a href="#entry-saptar_si" class="skt-link">saptarṣi</a>] Bharadvāja «Qui tient la Force (protectrice)»; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] il est dit fils d'<a href="#entry-Uatri" class="skt-link">Atri</a>; il fut le disciple de <a href="#entry-Uvaalmiiki" class="skt-link">Vālmīki</a>, et l'accompagnait lorsqu'ils virent le couple de courlis [<a href="#entry-krau_nca" class="skt-link">krauñca</a>] endeuillé par le chasseur; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> lui accorda des milliers d'années d'étude du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>, et lui enseigna l'<a href="#entry-aayurveda" class="skt-link">āyurveda</a>, qu'il transmis au sage <a href="#entry-Uaatreya" class="skt-link">Ātreya</a>; son ermitage [<a href="#entry-aazrama" class="skt-link">āśrama</a>] se trouvait à <a href="#entry-Uprayaaga" class="skt-link">Prayāga</a>; <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> s'y arrêta avec <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> et <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a> lors de son exil; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] faisant ses ablutions au bord du Gange, il répandit sa semence à la vue de la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] <a href="#entry-Ugh_rtaacii" class="skt-link">Ghṛtācī</a>, et la recueillit dans une coupe à ablutions [<a href="#entry-dro_na" class="skt-link">droṇa</a>], où naquit <a href="#entry-Udro_na" class="skt-link">Droṇa</a>; son épouse est <a href="#entry-Uzrutavatii" class="skt-link">Śrutavatī</a>, qui lui donna son fils <a href="#entry-Uzunahotra" class="skt-link">Śunahotra</a>; ami du roi <a href="#entry-Up_r_sata" class="skt-link">Pṛṣata</a>, il fut le précepteur [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] de son fils <a href="#entry-Udrupada" class="skt-link">Drupada</a>; [BhP.] après des milliers d'années d'étude du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>, il demanda une extension à <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, qui lui apparut devant trois montagnes et prit trois poignées de sable, qu'il compara à sa connaissance infime du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> par rapport aux trois montagnes; <a href="#entry-Ubharadvaaja" class="skt-link">Bharadvāja</a> ne se découragea pas, et continua son étude; on lui doit des hymnes du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. véd. np. de Bharadvāja_1 «né de (celle qui était) doublement enceinte», épith. de <a href="#entry-Udiirghatamaa" class="skt-link">Dīrghatamā</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Umamataa" class="skt-link">Mamatā</a> enceinte violée par son beau-frère <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>; sa mère l'abandonna dans la forêt, les génies des vents [<a href="#entry-marutas" class="skt-link">marutas</a>] le recueillirent et le donnèrent à <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a> qui le nomma son héritier <a href="#entry-Uvitatha" class="skt-link">Vitatha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bharad" class="skt-link">bharad</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भरत्</span> <i>bharat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bharat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bharat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bharantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bharantī</i> portant, soutenant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भरत</span> <i>bharata</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-bharata" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. «qui doit être entretenu (par l'homme)», épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> acrobate, danseur, acteur, mime <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'empereur [<a href="#entry-cakravartin" class="skt-link">cakravartin</a>] Bharata, roi de la lignée lunaire [<a href="#entry-somava_mza" class="skt-link">somavaṃśa</a>], fils de <a href="#entry-Udu_syanta" class="skt-link">Duṣyanta</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uzakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Śakuntalā</a>, ancêtre de <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a> et des guerriers <a href="#entry-Ubharataas" class="skt-link">Bharatās</a>; il donna son nom à l'Inde et aux indiens [<a href="#entry-bhaarata" class="skt-link">bhārata</a>]; avant d'être reconnu par son père, il vécut à l'ermitage de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> qui le nomma <a href="#entry-Usarvadamana" class="skt-link">Sarvadamana</a> en reconnaissance de son courage; devenu empereur [<a href="#entry-cakravartin" class="skt-link">cakravartin</a>], il conquit le monde en établissant la justice; il avait trois femmes, mais leurs fils étant malformés elles les tuèrent tous; il pria les dieux d'avoir un fils, et adopta le fils issu du viol de <a href="#entry-Umamataa" class="skt-link">Mamatā</a> par <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>, que celle-ci avait abandonné dans la forêt, et que les dieux recueillirent sous le nom de <a href="#entry-Ubharadvaaja_1" class="skt-link">Bharadvāja_1</a>; <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a> le nomma <a href="#entry-Uvitatha" class="skt-link">Vitatha</a> et en fit son héritier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du prince Bharata_1 fils de <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukaikeyii" class="skt-link">Kaikeyī</a>, demi-frère de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>; il fut régent du royaume en son absence, par la promesse que son père avait faite à <a href="#entry-Ukaikeyii" class="skt-link">Kaikeyī</a> que son fils règnerait; son compagnon était <a href="#entry-Uzatrughna" class="skt-link">Śatrughna</a>; il eut pour fils <a href="#entry-Utak_saa" class="skt-link">Takṣā</a> et <a href="#entry-Upu_skala" class="skt-link">Puṣkala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. np. de Bharata_2, fils de <a href="#entry-U_r_sabhanaatha" class="skt-link">Ṛṣabhanātha</a>, qui fit la guerre à son frère <a href="#entry-Ugomma_ta" class="skt-link">Gommaṭa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a> Bharata_3, dramaturge antique, à qui l'on attribue l'invention de l'art de la danse [<a href="#entry-bharatazaastra" class="skt-link">bharataśāstra</a>] et la composition du <a href="#entry-Unaa_tyazaastra" class="skt-link">Nāṭyaśāstra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> Bharata_4, fils de <a href="#entry-Udhruvasandhi" class="skt-link">Dhruvasandhi</a>, père d'<a href="#entry-Uasita" class="skt-link">Asita</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>bharatās</i> véd. les Bharatās, clan des descendants de <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a>; ce sont des <a href="#entry-Upauravaas" class="skt-link">Pauravās</a>, issus du peuple <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">ārya</a>; <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a> en était issu; les <a href="#entry-Ukauravaas" class="skt-link">Kauravās</a> et les <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍavās</a> aussi, qui se livrèrent à la guerre fratricide contée dans le <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharataar_nava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharatārṇava</i> [<a href="#entry-ar_nava" class="skt-link">arṇava</a>] <a href="#entry-bharataar_nava" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du Bharatārṇava «Tourbillon indien», traité complet de danse selon <a href="#entry-Ubharata_3" class="skt-link">Bharata_3</a>, composé par <a href="#entry-Unandikezvara" class="skt-link">Nandikeśvara</a> à la demande d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> voulant surpasser <a href="#entry-Una_tazekhara" class="skt-link">Naṭaśekhara</a>; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> en demanda un condensé, et obtint l'<a href="#entry-Uabhinayadarpa_na" class="skt-link">Abhinayadarpaṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bharata" class="skt-link">bharata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharatakathaama_njarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharatakathāmañjarī</i> [<a href="#entry-kathaa" class="skt-link">kathā</a>-<a href="#entry-ma_njarii" class="skt-link">mañjarī</a>] <a href="#entry-bharatakathaama_njarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. du poème Barathakathāmañjarī, version du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> due à <a href="#entry-Uk_semendra" class="skt-link">Kṣemendra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharatakha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharatakhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-bharatakha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«partie de <a href="#entry-bharatavar_sa" class="skt-link">bharatavarṣa</a>»] province de l'Inde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharatakuupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharatakūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-kuupa" class="skt-link">kūpa</a>] <a href="#entry-bharatakuupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. puits du prince Bharata_1 à <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a>; son eau sert à célébrer son couronnement lors de la <a href="#entry-raamaliilaa" class="skt-link">rāmalīlā</a> annuelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharatakuupajananii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharatakūpajananī</i> [<a href="#entry-jananii" class="skt-link">jananī</a>] <a href="#entry-bharatakuupajananii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. «mère puits», symboliquement mariée au pilier Lāṭ Bhairo symbolisant le <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> de <a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bharatakuupa" class="skt-link">bharatakūpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharatamuni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharatamuni</i> [<a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a>] <a href="#entry-bharatamuni" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bharatamuni, inventeur de l'art de la danse; on lui attribue le <a href="#entry-Unaa_tyazaastra" class="skt-link">Nāṭyaśāstra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubharata_3" class="skt-link">Bharata_3</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharatar_sabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharatarṣabha</i> [<a href="#entry-_r_sabha" class="skt-link">ṛṣabha</a>] <a href="#entry-bharatar_sabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Bharatarṣabha «meilleur parmi les <a href="#entry-Ubharataas" class="skt-link">Bharatās</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharatavaakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharatavākya</i> [<a href="#entry-vaakya" class="skt-link">vākya</a>] <a href="#entry-bharatavaakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [«récitation par un acteur»] bénédiction finale appelant la grâce de la divinité sur le poète, le roi et ses sujets, dite à la fin d'une représentation théâtrale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharatavar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharatavarṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-bharatavar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. [«pays de <a href="#entry-Ubharata" class="skt-link">Bharata</a>»] Inde, l'un des 9 bassins versants [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] du monde; cf. <a href="#entry-jambudviipa" class="skt-link">jambudvīpa</a>, <a href="#entry-bhaaratavar_sa" class="skt-link">bhāratavarṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharatazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharataśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-bharatazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. manuel de l'art de la danse classique [<a href="#entry-naa_tyazaastra" class="skt-link">nāṭyaśāstra</a>], attribué à <a href="#entry-Ubharata_3" class="skt-link">Bharata_3</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भर्ग</span> <i>bharga</i> [<a href="#entry-bhraaj" class="skt-link">bhrāj</a>] <a href="#entry-bharga" class="skt-link">m.</a> éclat, splendeur; effulgence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bharga, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Resplendissant» — <a href="#entry-bhargas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>bhargas</i> radiance; splendeur, gloire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>ṅulgur</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhart_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भर्तृ</span> <i>bhartṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhart_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhart_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhartrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhartrī</i> qui supporte, qui préserve, qui maintient ⟨iic. g.⟩; qui entretient, qui nourrit — <a href="#entry-bhart_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> maître, patron, chef; mari — <a href="#entry-bhartrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhartrī</i> mère nourricière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhart_rhari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhartṛhari</i> [<a href="#entry-hari_2" class="skt-link">hari_2</a>] <a href="#entry-bhart_rhari" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhartṛhari «Maître Hari», philosophe de la langue et poète d'<a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a> (?580–651); il est l'auteur d'un important traité de philosophie de la langue <a href="#entry-Uvaakyapadiiya" class="skt-link">Vākyapadīya</a> avec son commentaire [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>], et du commentaire <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaa_syadiipikaa" class="skt-link">Mahābhāṣyadīpikā</a> sur le <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mahābhāṣya</a> de <a href="#entry-Upata_njali_1" class="skt-link">Patañjali_1</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uhariv_r_sabha" class="skt-link">Harivṛṣabha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du poète Bhartṛhari_1, frère présumé du roi <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya</a>; il revint sept fois sur sa vocation bouddhiste; il composa trois centuries [<a href="#entry-Uzatakatraya" class="skt-link">Śatakatraya</a>] de poèmes, resp. sur l'érotique [<a href="#entry-z_rfgaara" class="skt-link">śṛṅgāra</a>], la morale [<a href="#entry-niiti" class="skt-link">nīti</a>], et le renoncement [<a href="#entry-vairaagya" class="skt-link">vairāgya</a>]; on montre à <a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a> les caves où il aurait vécu comme ermite.<br> <a href="#entry-bhart_r" class="skt-link">bhartṛ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhart_rmatii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhartṛmatī</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] <a href="#entry-bhart_rmatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> femme mariée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhart_rpi_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhartṛpiṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-pi_n_da" class="skt-link">piṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhart_rpi_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhart_rpi_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. salaire payé par le maître à ses serviteurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhart_rprapa_nca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhartṛprapañca</i> [<a href="#entry-prapa_nca" class="skt-link">prapañca</a>] <a href="#entry-bhart_rprapa_nca" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe Bhartṛprapañca, l'un des premiers commentateurs du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a>, antérieur à <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara_1</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-j_naanakarmasamuccayavaada" class="skt-link">jñānakarmasamuccayavāda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhart_rsneha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhartṛsneha</i> [<a href="#entry-sneha" class="skt-link">sneha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhart_rsneha" class="skt-link">m.</a> faveur du maître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharts">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भर्त्स्</span> <i>bharts</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(bhartsayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhartsita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i>, <i>nis</i><i>)</i> menacer, injurier; gronder, railler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhartsita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भर्त्सित</span> <i>bhartsita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bharts" class="skt-link">bharts</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhartsita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhartsita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhartsitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhartsitā</i> menacé, injurié; grondé — <a href="#entry-bhartsita" class="skt-link">n.</a> menace.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भरु</span> <i>bharu</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-bharu" class="skt-link">m.</a> seigneur, maître; époux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bharu «Seigneur», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharuka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-bharuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Bharuka, de la lignée solaire [<a href="#entry-suuryava_mza" class="skt-link">sūryavaṃśa</a>], fils de <a href="#entry-Usudeva" class="skt-link">Sudeva</a>; <a href="#entry-Ubaahuka" class="skt-link">Bāhuka</a> est son fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bharukaccha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bharukaccha</i> [<a href="#entry-kaccha" class="skt-link">kaccha</a>] <a href="#entry-bharukaccha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bharukaccha" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du pays et de la ville de Bharukaccha, corruption de <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgukaccha" class="skt-link">Bhṛgukaccha</a> «Rivage du Seigneur (<a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a>)», à l'embouchure de la <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a>; mod. Broach/Baruch au Gujarāt.<br> <a href="#entry-bharu" class="skt-link">bharu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भस्</span> <i>bhas</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(babhasti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhasita</i><i>)</i> mastiquer, dévorer; consumer, réduire en cendres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhasad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भसद्</span> <i>bhasad</i> <a href="#entry-bhasad" class="skt-link">f.</a> postérieur, fesses <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> parties génitales; gland du pénis.<br> <i>ābhasad</i> en dessous des hanches.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhasattas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhasattas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] ind. en dessous du fessier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jusqu'aux fesses.<br> <a href="#entry-bhasad" class="skt-link">bhasad</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भसित</span> <i>bhasita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhas" class="skt-link">bhas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhasitā</i> consumé, réduit en cendres — <a href="#entry-bhasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> cendres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhasma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भस्म</span> <i>bhasma</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-bhasman" class="skt-link">bhasman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhasmaafkura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhasmāṅkura</i> [<a href="#entry-afkura" class="skt-link">aṅkura</a>] <a href="#entry-bhasmaafkura" class="skt-link">m.</a> progéniture d'un ascète <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a> et d'une prostituée <a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhasmaasura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhasmāsura</i> [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] <a href="#entry-bhasmaasura" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhasmāsura, l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> «des Cendres»; il obtint de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> une baguette magique permettant de réduire en cendres tout être touché par elle; il chercha à détruire <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> avec, mais fut trompé par <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> qui l'incita à l'essayer sur lui-même pour constater qu'elle était inopérante.<br> <a href="#entry-bhasma" class="skt-link">bhasma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhasman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भस्मन्</span> <i>bhasman</i> [<a href="#entry-bhas" class="skt-link">bhas</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-bhasman" class="skt-link">n.</a> cendres.<br> <i>bhasmīkṛ</i> réduire en cendres.<br> <i>bhasmībhū</i> être réduit en cendres.<br> <i>bhasmībhūtā</i> réduit en cendres.<br> <i>bhasmībhāva</i> condition d'être réduit en cendres.<br> <i>bhasmībhāvaṃ</i> <i>gataḥ</i> réduit en cendres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhasmasaat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhasmasāt</i> [<a href="#entry--saat" class="skt-link">-sāt</a>] ind. (réduit) en cendres (avec <a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>, <a href="#entry-as_1" class="skt-link">as_1</a>, <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>, <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhastraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भस्त्रा</span> <i>bhastrā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhas" class="skt-link">bhas</a><a href="#entry--tra" class="skt-link">-tra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhastraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> outre de peau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sac de cuir; gourde en cuir; soufflet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>bhastrī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhastrika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भस्त्रिक</span> <i>bhastrika</i> [<a href="#entry-bhastraa" class="skt-link">bhastrā</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhastrika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhastrika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhastrikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhastrikī</i> qui emmène dans une outre — <a href="#entry-bhastrika" class="skt-link">m.</a> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] technique de respiration intensive [<a href="#entry-praa_naayaama" class="skt-link">prāṇāyāma</a>] — <a href="#entry-bhastrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhastrikā</i> petit sac de cuir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhauma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भौम</span> <i>bhauma</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">bhūmi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhauma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhauma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaumii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaumī</i> relatif à la terre, terrestre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de terre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (revenu) foncier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relatif à la planète Mars; qui se produit le Mardi — <a href="#entry-bhauma" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhauma, épith. de <a href="#entry-Umafgala" class="skt-link">Maṅgala</a> «issu de la Terre» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bhauma_1, patr. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Unaraka" class="skt-link">Naraka</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Ubhuumi" class="skt-link">Bhūmi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. ère [<a href="#entry-sa_mvat" class="skt-link">saṃvat</a>] de la dynastie <a href="#entry-Ubhaumakara" class="skt-link">Bhaumakara</a> — <a href="#entry-bhaumii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaumī</i> myth. np. de Bhaumī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> «issue de la Terre».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaumakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaumakara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaumakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de la dynastie Bhaumakara, établie en 736 en Orissa; de nombreux monastères bd. furent institués à son début; Jajpur était sa capitale; au 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle la dynastie <a href="#entry-Usomava_mzii" class="skt-link">Somavaṃśī</a> lui succéda.<br> <a href="#entry-bhauma" class="skt-link">bhauma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhauta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
