    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भौत</span> <i>bhauta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhauta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhauta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhautii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhautī</i> qui concerne les êtres vivants; qui concerne les substances <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> matériel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relatif aux esprits; possédé par des démons; dément, fou; idiot — <a href="#entry-bhauta" class="skt-link">n.</a> la multitude des esprits — <a href="#entry-bhautii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhautī</i> nuit [«où rôdent les esprits»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhautaakhyaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhautākhyāna</i> [<a href="#entry-aakhyaana" class="skt-link">ākhyāna</a>] <a href="#entry-bhautaakhyaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du genre littéraire <a href="#entry-bhautaakhyaana" class="skt-link">bhautākhyāna</a> ou <a href="#entry-bhautopaakhyaana" class="skt-link">bhautopākhyāna</a>; il consiste en anecdotes satiriques mettant en scène des personnages stupides, représentant les opposants des auteurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhautazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhautaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhautazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> mod. la science physique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhautika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भौतिक</span> <i>bhautika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhautika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhautika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhautikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhautikī</i> phil. dérivé des éléments naturels; matériel — <a href="#entry-bhautika" class="skt-link">n.</a> perle — <a href="#entry-bhautikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhautikī</i> mod. physique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhautopaakhyaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhautopākhyāna</i> [<a href="#entry-upaakhyaana" class="skt-link">upākhyāna</a>] <a href="#entry-bhautopaakhyaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-bhautaakhyaana" class="skt-link">bhautākhyāna</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhauta" class="skt-link">bhauta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhautya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भौत्य</span> <i>bhautya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhuuti" class="skt-link">bhūti</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhautya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhautya «Fortuné», 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a> du <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भव</span> <i>bhava</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> existence, état; origine, naissance; prospérité, excellence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> obtention, acquisition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le monde, la vie ici-bas <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. qualité du sentiment exprimé par l'artiste et ressenti par le spectateur de l'œuvre artistique, l'un des 6 canons esthétiques des arts plastiques [<a href="#entry-_sa_dafgazilpa" class="skt-link">ṣaḍaṅgaśilpa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. continuité de l'existence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bhava «l'Être», un nom de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>bhavau</i> myth. <a href="#entry-Ubhava" class="skt-link">Bhava</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubhavaanii" class="skt-link">Bhavānī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adr. (imp.) sois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavaanii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भवानी</span> <i>bhavānī</i> [<a href="#entry-bhava" class="skt-link">bhava</a><a href="#entry--aanii" class="skt-link">-ānī</a>] <a href="#entry-bhavaanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Bhavānī «Existence», forme pacifique de la Déesse; épith. de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ubhava" class="skt-link">Bhava</a>-<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavaaniizafkara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhavānīśaṅkara</i> [<a href="#entry-zafkara" class="skt-link">śaṅkara</a>] <a href="#entry-bhavaaniizafkara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhavānīśaṅkara «<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> avec <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>»; il est adoré au monastère [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>] de <a href="#entry-Ucitrapura" class="skt-link">Citrapura</a> au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhavaanii" class="skt-link">bhavānī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavabhuuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhavabhūti</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuuti" class="skt-link">bhūti</a>] <a href="#entry-bhavabhuuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Bhavabhūti «Prospère», poète et dramaturge du 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il naquit à Padmapura au <a href="#entry-Uvidarbha" class="skt-link">Vidarbha</a>; il vécut à la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uyazovarmaa" class="skt-link">Yaśovarmā</a> à <a href="#entry-Ukanyaakubjaa" class="skt-link">Kanyākubjā</a>, et portait le nom de <a href="#entry-Uzriika_n_tha_1" class="skt-link">Śrīkaṇṭha_1</a> <a href="#entry-Uniilaka_n_tha_1" class="skt-link">Nīlakaṇṭha_1</a>; il est l'auteur not. du <a href="#entry-Umahaaviiracarita" class="skt-link">Mahāvīracarita</a>, de l'<a href="#entry-Uuttararaamacarita" class="skt-link">Uttararāmacarita</a> et du <a href="#entry-Umaalatiimaadhava" class="skt-link">Mālatīmādhava</a>; on le range avec <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a> parmi les gloires de la poésie sanskrite.<br> <a href="#entry-bhava" class="skt-link">bhava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavacakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhavacakra</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhavacakra" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. [«roue de l'existence»] représentation (not. au Tibet) du cycle des renaissances [<a href="#entry-sa_msaara" class="skt-link">saṃsāra</a>]; ce diagramme circulaire a 6 parties, représentant les règnes respectifs des dieux [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>], des démons [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>], des spectres [<a href="#entry-preta" class="skt-link">preta</a>], des créatures infernales [<a href="#entry-naraka" class="skt-link">naraka</a>], des animaux [<a href="#entry-tiryac" class="skt-link">tiryac</a>] et des hommes [<a href="#entry-manu_sya" class="skt-link">manuṣya</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-sa_msaaracakra" class="skt-link">saṃsāracakra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavacchid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhavacchid</i> [<a href="#entry-chid_2" class="skt-link">chid_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavacchid" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavacchid" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavacchid" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. qui coupe net la chaîne des transmigrations.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भवद्</span> <i>bhavad</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-bhavat_2" class="skt-link">bhavat_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavadiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhavadīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavadiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavadiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavadiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhavadīyā</i> votre, le vôtre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adr. bien à vous.<br> <a href="#entry-bhavad" class="skt-link">bhavad</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भवन</span> <i>bhavana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> maison, demeure, résidence; palais — ifc. siège de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भवनीय</span> <i>bhavanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhavaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> (impers.) il faut que cela soit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavat_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भवत्</span> <i>bhavat_1</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavat_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavat_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhavantī</i> étant, existant, présent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavat_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भवत्</span> <i>bhavat_2</i> var. <i>bhavad</i> [<a href="#entry-bhagavat" class="skt-link">bhagavat</a>] <a href="#entry-bhavat_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> adr. Monsieur (présent, forme polie de tu/vous à la 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ pers.) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>bhavantas</i> Messieurs — <a href="#entry-bhavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhavatī</i> Madame (voc. <i>bhavati</i>).<br> <i>atrabhavat</i> ce Monsieur-ci (ici-présent, forme polie de il).<br> <i>tatrabhavat</i> ce Monsieur-là (non-présent, forme polie de il).<br> <i>ihabhavantaḥ</i> ce grand homme.<br> <i>bhavataḥ</i> <i>nāma</i> <i>kim</i> adr. comment vous appelez-vous? (à un homme, <i>bhavatyāḥ</i> à une femme).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavi_s_nu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भविष्णु</span> <i>bhaviṣṇu</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a><a href="#entry--i_s_nu" class="skt-link">-iṣṇu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavi_s_nu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavi_s_nu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavi_s_nu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui va se produire, imminent; futur — ifc. (après acc.) en train de devenir.<br> <i>andhaṃbhaviṣṇu</i> en train de devenir aveugle.<br> <i>āḍhyaṃbhaviṣṇu</i> en train de s'enrichir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavi_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भविष्य</span> <i>bhaviṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhavi_syat" class="skt-link">bhaviṣyat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavi_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavi_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavi_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaviṣyā</i> qui va se produire, imminent; futur — <a href="#entry-bhavi_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> le futur, l'avenir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavi_syapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhaviṣyapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhavi_syapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Bhaviṣyapurāṇa «Histoire du futur», composé entre 500 et 1200; son compilateur <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a> y conte le <a href="#entry-kaliyuga" class="skt-link">kaliyuga</a> selon la vision prophétique de <a href="#entry-Usuuta_1" class="skt-link">Sūta_1</a> <a href="#entry-Ugosvaamii" class="skt-link">Gosvāmī</a>; ce texte interprète la Genèse de la Bible, adaptant Adam [Adama] et Éve [Havyavatī], l'arbre du péché [<i>pāpavṛkṣa</i>], avec <a href="#entry-Ukali" class="skt-link">Kali</a> dans le rôle du serpent; ils engendrèrent la race des <a href="#entry-mlecchaas" class="skt-link">mlecchās</a>; Noah est Nyuha, <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> lui apparut en rêve au temps du Déluge, pour construire l'arche; <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya</a> est ensuite évoqué, puis Mahomet [Mahāmāda], illusioniste béni par <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; il apparut en songe au roi <a href="#entry-Ubhoja" class="skt-link">Bhoja</a> comme un fantôme [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>] pour annoncer qu'il fondait une religion impie; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> lui révéla que c'était le fantôme de <a href="#entry-Utripura" class="skt-link">Tripura</a>, revenu pour diffuser une fausse doctrine; le roi <a href="#entry-Ubhoja" class="skt-link">Bhoja</a> donna le <a href="#entry-sa_msk_rta" class="skt-link">saṃskṛta</a> aux aryens [<a href="#entry-dvija" class="skt-link">dvija</a>] et laissa le <a href="#entry-praak_rta" class="skt-link">prākṛta</a> aux <a href="#entry-zuudraas" class="skt-link">śūdrās</a>; puis les bouddhistes prospérèrent avant de partir pour la Chine; <a href="#entry-Ujayadeva" class="skt-link">Jayadeva</a> est présenté comme incarnation de <a href="#entry-Utva_s_taa" class="skt-link">Tvaṣṭā</a> composant le <a href="#entry-Unirukta" class="skt-link">Nirukta</a> puis le <a href="#entry-Ugiitagovinda" class="skt-link">Gītagovinda</a>; enfin les musulmans envahissent l'Inde et détruisent les temples.<br> <a href="#entry-bhavi_sya" class="skt-link">bhaviṣya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavi_syat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भविष्यत्</span> <i>bhaviṣyat</i> [pfu. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavi_syat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavi_syat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavi_syantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaviṣyantī</i> imminent; à venir; futur — <a href="#entry-bhavi_syat" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. temps du futur — <a href="#entry-bhavi_syantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhaviṣyantī</i> gram. marque du futur; cf. <a href="#entry-l_r_t" class="skt-link">lṛṭ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भविन्</span> <i>bhavin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhavinī</i> étant, vivant — <a href="#entry-bhavin" class="skt-link">m.</a> être vivant; homme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavit_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भवितृ</span> <i>bhavitṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavit_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhavitrī</i> qui va être, qui sera; futur, ultérieur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भवितव्य</span> <i>bhavitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhavitavyā</i> qui doit être, qui doit s'accomplir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavitavyataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhavitavyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhavitavyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> ce qui doit s'accomplir, l'inévitable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> destinée.<br> <a href="#entry-bhavitavya" class="skt-link">bhavitavya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भव्य</span> <i>bhavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhavyā</i> existant, présent, réel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui va s'accomplir, qui va se produire; futur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adéquat, opportun; bon, juste; favorable; heureux, plaisant; gracieux, excellent — <a href="#entry-bhavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> existence; avenir; résultat, récompense; succès — <a href="#entry-bhavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du lettré bd. Bhavya «Bienheureux» (~500–~578), élève de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a>, aussi appelé Bhavyaviveka; il est l'auteur de la Madhyamakahṛdayakārikā et de son commentaire le Tarkajvala <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> np. de nombreux personnages mythiques ou historiques.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भय</span> <i>bhaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> peur, crainte, effroi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> danger, péril, détresse — <a href="#entry-bhaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> maladie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bhaya l'«Effroi» personnifié, fils de <a href="#entry-Unir_rti" class="skt-link">Nirṛti</a> — loc. pl. cf. <a href="#entry-bhaya_su" class="skt-link">bhayaṣu</a>.<br> <i>bhayāt</i> adv. par peur.<br> <i>bhayaṣu</i> adv. dans les moments de peur.<br> <i>bhayaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> avoir peur (de ⟨abl.⟩).<br> <i>bhayaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> avoir peur.<br> <i>bhayaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> faire peur, effrayer, épouvanter.<br> <i>bhayādasyāgnistapati</i> <i>bhayāttapati</i> <i>sūryaḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>bhayādindraśca</i> <i>vāyuśca</i> <i>mṛtyurdhāvati</i> <i>pañcamaḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [KU.] Par peur de Lui, le feu brûle; par peur, le Soleil luit; par peur, aussi, <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a> et en cinquième, la Mort qui court.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhayaakula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhayākula</i> [<a href="#entry-aakula" class="skt-link">ākula</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhayaakula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayaakula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayaakulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhayākulā</i> affolé de peur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhayaanaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhayānaka</i> a. <a href="#entry-bhayaanaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayaanaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayaanakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhayānakā</i> effrayant, épouvantable — <a href="#entry-bhayaanaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> terreur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'effroyable, un des 9 modes artistiques [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>]; on lui associe la couleur noire et la divinité <a href="#entry-Ukaala_2" class="skt-link">Kāla_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhayaatura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhayātura</i> [<a href="#entry-aatura" class="skt-link">ātura</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhayaatura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayaatura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayaaturaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhayāturā</i> malade de peur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhayaavaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhayāvaha</i> [<a href="#entry-aavaha" class="skt-link">āvaha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhayaavaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayaavaha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayaavahaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhayāvahā</i> effrayant, terrible.<br> <a href="#entry-bhaya" class="skt-link">bhaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhayafkara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhayaṃkara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhaya_mkara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaya_mkara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhaya_mkaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhayaṃkarā</i> terrible, qui fait peur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhayasamupeta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhayasamupeta</i> [<a href="#entry-samupeta" class="skt-link">samupeta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhayasamupeta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayasamupeta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayasamupetaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhayasamupetā</i> pris de peur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhayya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भय्य</span> <i>bhayya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhayya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhayyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhayyā</i> propre à être craint — <a href="#entry-bhayya" class="skt-link">n.</a> (impers.) ⟨abl.⟩ est à craindre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_nj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भञ्ज्</span> <i>bhañj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(bhanakti)</i> pft. <i>(babhañja)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhagna</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> briser, casser, réduire en morceaux, détruire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> battre, disperser, vaincre, anéantir — ps. <i>(bhajyate)</i> être détruit — ca. <i>(bhañjayati)</i> provoquer la destruction de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>frango</i>; fr. briser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_njaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भञ्जक</span> <i>bhañjaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bha_nj" class="skt-link">bhañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bha_njaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_njaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_njikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhañjikā</i> qui brise, qui détruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en posture déhanchée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_njana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भञ्जन</span> <i>bhañjana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bha_nj" class="skt-link">bhañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bha_njana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_njanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhañjanā</i> destructeur — <a href="#entry-bha_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> destruction, démolition; interruption — <a href="#entry-bha_njana" class="skt-link">m.</a> carie; perte de dents.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bha_njayat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भञ्जयत्</span> <i>bhañjayat</i> [ca. ppr. <a href="#entry-bha_nj" class="skt-link">bhañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bha_njayat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_njayat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bha_njayantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhañjayantī</i> faisant détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhe_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेड</span> <i>bheḍa</i> <a href="#entry-bhe_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> bélier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>bheṇḍa</i> id. — <a href="#entry-bhe_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bheḍī</i> brebis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhe_saja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेषज</span> <i>bheṣaja</i> [<a href="#entry-bhi_saj" class="skt-link">bhiṣaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhe_saja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhe_saja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhe_sajii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bheṣajī</i> qui guérit, qui soigne — <a href="#entry-bhe_saja" class="skt-link">n.</a> médicament; remède (contre ⟨g. iic.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> charme de guérison, not. de l'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaveda" class="skt-link">Atharvaveda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेद</span> <i>bheda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> fente, brisure; fissure (du. lèvres du vagin) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> destruction, violation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> effraction; divulgation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> état d'être brisé; froncement (des sourcils) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> séparation; différence; intervalle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> classification <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> intrigue, zizanie; corruption, trahison <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. hypothénuse d'un triangle rectangle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. dualité entre Dieu et l'Univers (opp. monisme [<a href="#entry-abheda" class="skt-link">abheda</a>]) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. violation d'une règle; ambiguité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhedaabheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhedābheda</i> [<a href="#entry-abheda" class="skt-link">abheda</a>] <a href="#entry-bhedaabheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. phil. dualité et monisme; [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] école professant que le <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> et la création sont à la fois séparés et liés.<br> <i>acintya</i> <i>bhedābheda</i> <i>tattva</i> inconcevable simultanéité de l'unité et de la différence entre Dieu et l'âme individuelle (résumé de la doctrine de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhedaabhedavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhedābhedavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-bhedaabhedavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. doctrine professant à la fois la dualité et le monisme; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhaaskara_1" class="skt-link">Bhāskara_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhedaabheda" class="skt-link">bhedābheda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhedaagraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhedāgraha</i> [<a href="#entry-agraha" class="skt-link">agraha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhedaagraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [bd.] non appréhension de la différence; opp. <a href="#entry-abhedagraha" class="skt-link">abhedagraha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhedaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेदक</span> <i>bhedaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhedaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhedaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhedikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhedikā</i> qui troue, qui perfore; qui détruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui distingue, qui détermine, qui définit — <a href="#entry-bhedaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. adjectif — <a href="#entry-bhedikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhedikā</i> destruction, annihilation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhedana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेदन</span> <i>bhedana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhedana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de fendre, de briser, de séparer; division, rupture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de trahir (not. un secret ⟨iic.⟩); fait de semer la discorde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhedavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhedavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-bhedavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. doctrine de dualité entre Dieu et l'Univers.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhedojjiivana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhedojjīvana</i> [<a href="#entry-ujjiivana" class="skt-link">ujjīvana</a>] <a href="#entry-bhedojjiivana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Bhedojjīvana «Renaissance de la différence», petit ouvrage de <a href="#entry-Uvyaasatiirtha" class="skt-link">Vyāsatīrtha</a> s'opposant au monisme.<br> <a href="#entry-bheda" class="skt-link">bheda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhedya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेद्य</span> <i>bhedya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhedya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhedya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhedyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhedyā</i> qui doît être cassé, fendu ou perforé; à ouvrir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à contester, à réfuter; à analyser, à déterminer — <a href="#entry-bhedya" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. substantif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bheka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेक</span> <i>bheka</i> <a href="#entry-bheka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. grenouille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bherii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेरी</span> <i>bherī</i> <a href="#entry-bherii" class="skt-link">f.</a> timbale, grosse caisse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bheru_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेरुण्ड</span> <i>bheruṇḍa</i> var. <i>bheraṇḍa</i> a. <a href="#entry-bheru_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bheru_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bheru_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bheruṇḍā</i> terrible, formidable, terrifiant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> oiseau de proie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> animal carnassier (loup, chacal, renard, hyène).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhetavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेतव्य</span> <i>bhetavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhetavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhetavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhetavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhetavyā</i> à craindre, que l'on doit redouter.<br> <i>na</i> <i>bhetavyam</i> (impers.) il ne faut pas avoir peur; n'aie pas peur (de ⟨abl. g.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bheya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भेय</span> <i>bheya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bheya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bheya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bheyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bheyā</i> à craindre, que l'on doit redouter — acc. <i>bheyam</i> (impers.) il faut craindre ⟨abl.⟩.<br> <i>arer</i> <i>bheyam</i> il faut craindre l'ennemi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhi_saj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिषज्</span> <i>bhiṣaj</i> <a href="#entry-bhi_saj" class="skt-link">m.</a> médecin, guérisseur — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(bhiṣajyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhiṣajyita</i><i>)</i> traiter (méd.); guérir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être un remède pour, être maître de ⟨loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhi_sajya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिषज्य</span> <i>bhiṣajya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhi_saj" class="skt-link">bhiṣaj</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhi_sajya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhi_sajya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhi_sajyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhiṣajyā</i> qui guérit; salubre; sain, en bonne santé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhi_sajyita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिषज्यित</span> <i>bhiṣajyita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhi_saj" class="skt-link">bhiṣaj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhi_sajyita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhi_sajyita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhi_sajyitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhiṣajyitā</i> guéri.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhid_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिद्</span> <i>bhid_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(bhinatti)</i> pr. md. <i>(bhintte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhinna</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhedya</i>, <i>bhidya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>bhittvā</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> fendre, briser, percer, fracturer; violer; séparer, interrompre; détruire — ps. <i>(bhidyate)</i> se fendre — ca. <i>(bhedayati)</i> ca. md. <i>(bhedayate)</i> fendre, briser; détruire; diviser; vaincre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>findere</i>; all. beissen; ang. to bite; fr. fendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhid_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिद्</span> <i>bhid_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-bhid_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhid_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhid_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui perce, qui fend, qui détruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhidaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिदा</span> <i>bhidā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhidaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> destruction, éclatement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhidya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिद्य</span> <i>bhidya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhidya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhidya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhidyā</i> qui détruit — <a href="#entry-bhidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhidyā</i> rivière impétueuse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भीष्</span> <i>bhīṣ</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. md. <i>(bhīṣayate)</i> faire peur, effrayer, terrifier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_sma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भीष्म</span> <i>bhīṣma</i> [<a href="#entry-bhii_s" class="skt-link">bhīṣ</a><a href="#entry--ma" class="skt-link">-ma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhii_sma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhii_sma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhii_smaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhīṣmā</i> terrible, effrayant — <a href="#entry-bhii_sma" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Bhīṣma «le Terrible», surnom du prince <a href="#entry-Udevavrata" class="skt-link">Devavrata</a>, fils aîné du roi <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāṃtanu</a> et de la déesse <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a>, et comme tel, don du Ciel [<a href="#entry-devadatta" class="skt-link">devadatta</a>]; il est l'incarnation du <a href="#entry-vasu" class="skt-link">vasu</a> Ciel [<a href="#entry-Udyau" class="skt-link">Dyau</a>]; son <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> étant <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a>, il devint invincible aux armes; pour permettre à son père d'épouser la belle <a href="#entry-Usatyavatii" class="skt-link">Satyavatī</a> en lui promettant d'enfanter l'héritier du royaume, il renonça au trône et fit vœu de <a href="#entry-brahmacarya" class="skt-link">brahmacarya</a>; son père le récompensa en lui donnant le pouvoir de choisir l'heure de sa mort; après la mort tragique du premier héritier <a href="#entry-Ucitraafgada" class="skt-link">Citrāṅgada</a>, il plaça sur le trône le jeune <a href="#entry-Uvicitraviirya" class="skt-link">Vicitravīrya</a>; à sa majorité, il lui choisit ses épouses par rapt des filles du roi de <a href="#entry-Ukaazi" class="skt-link">Kāśi</a> lors d'une cérémonie de <a href="#entry-svaya_mvara" class="skt-link">svayaṃvara</a>; l'aînée, <a href="#entry-Uambaa" class="skt-link">Ambā</a>, étant promise au roi <a href="#entry-Uzaalva" class="skt-link">Śālva</a>, il maria <a href="#entry-Uvicitraviirya" class="skt-link">Vicitravīrya</a> aux deux cadettes <a href="#entry-Uambikaa_1" class="skt-link">Ambikā_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Uambaalikaa_1" class="skt-link">Ambālikā_1</a>; pour avoir provoqué la répudiation d'<a href="#entry-Uambaa" class="skt-link">Ambā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a> le combattit sans succès dans un combat titanesque de 23 jours; à la mort de <a href="#entry-Uvicitraviirya" class="skt-link">Vicitravīrya</a> sans héritier, il maria les deux veuves à l'ascète <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a> que <a href="#entry-Usatyavatii" class="skt-link">Satyavatī</a> avait enfanté autrefois du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-Uparaazara" class="skt-link">Parāśara</a>; l'aîné de cette union <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a> étant aveugle, il couronna le cadet <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_du" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍu</a>; lorsque celui-ci renonça au trône, le royaume fut disputé entre ses 5 fils, les <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>, et leurs cousins <a href="#entry-kauravaas" class="skt-link">kauravās</a>, menés par <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a>, fils aîné de <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a>; <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>, l'invincible patriarche des <a href="#entry-kauravaas" class="skt-link">kauravās</a>, et leur commandant en chef, fut blessé mortellement par <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> accompagné de <a href="#entry-Uzikha_n_dii" class="skt-link">Śikhaṇḍī</a>, car il ne voulait pas combattre une femme (réincarnation d'<a href="#entry-Uambaa" class="skt-link">Ambā</a>); il attendit la fin de la guerre, couché sur son lit de flèches, pour s'éteindre à l'<a href="#entry-uttaraaya_na" class="skt-link">uttarāyaṇa</a>, après avoir prodigué sa sagesse aux <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_smaa_s_tamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīṣmāṣṭamī</i> [<a href="#entry-a_s_tamii" class="skt-link">aṣṭamī</a>] <a href="#entry-bhii_smaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a>; c'est une fête en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhii_sma" class="skt-link">bhīṣma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_smaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भीष्मक</span> <i>bhīṣmaka</i> [<a href="#entry-bhii_sma" class="skt-link">bhīṣma</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-bhii_smaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi Bhīṣmaka, père de <a href="#entry-Urukmii" class="skt-link">Rukmī</a> et <a href="#entry-Urukmi_nii" class="skt-link">Rukmiṇī</a>; il régnait sur le <a href="#entry-Uvidarbha" class="skt-link">Vidarbha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_smaparvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīṣmaparvan</i> [<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>] <a href="#entry-bhii_smaparvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, «traitant de <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>»; il conte les combats et la mort du patriarche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_smapa_ncaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīṣmapañcaka</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaka" class="skt-link">pañcaka</a>] <a href="#entry-bhii_smapa_ncaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. 5 jours sacrés à <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a> (du 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ au 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-kaarttika" class="skt-link">kārttika</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_smapa_ncakavrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīṣmapañcakavrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] <a href="#entry-bhii_smapa_ncakavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. observance suivie dans cette période.<br> <a href="#entry-bhii_smapa_ncaka" class="skt-link">bhīṣmapañcaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_smastavaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīṣmastavarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-stava" class="skt-link">stava</a>-<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-bhii_smastavaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Bhīṣmastavarāja «Hymne de louanges à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> de <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a> (mourant sur son lit de flèches)», épisode du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> [12-47].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भी</span> <i>bhī_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(bibheti)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] <i>(abhaiṣīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhīta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhetavya</i>, <i>bheya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> craindre ⟨abl. g.⟩; être effrayé — ca. <i>(bhīṣayati)</i> ca. md. <i>(bhīṣayate)</i> ca. <i>(bhāyayati)</i> ca. md. <i>(bhāyayate)</i> faire peur, effrayer, terrifier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. φοβος; all. beben; fr. phobie.<br> <i>mābhaiṣīḥ</i> var. <i>mābhaiḥ</i> n'aie pas peur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhii_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भी</span> <i>bhī_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhii_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> peur, crainte, effroi, terreur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiima">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भीम</span> <i>bhīma</i> [<a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a><a href="#entry--ma" class="skt-link">-ma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhiima" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhiima" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhiimaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhīmā</i> redoutable; terrible, épouvantable, formidable — <a href="#entry-bhiima" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Bhīma «Terrifiant», frère d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhiimasena" class="skt-link">Bhīmasena</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>-<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi Bhīma_1 du pays <a href="#entry-Uvidarbha" class="skt-link">Vidarbha</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Udamayantii" class="skt-link">Damayantī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du roi <a href="#entry-Ucaalukya" class="skt-link">Cālukya</a> Bhīma_2 (892–921).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiimaacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīmācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhiimaacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-mahaamahopaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">mahāmahopādhyāya</a> Bhīmācārya, auteur du <a href="#entry-Unyaayakoza" class="skt-link">Nyāyakośa</a> (19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiimaparaakrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīmaparākrama</i> [<a href="#entry-paraakrama" class="skt-link">parākrama</a>] <a href="#entry-bhiimaparaakrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> courage ou force terrible — a. <a href="#entry-bhiimaparaakrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhiimaparaakrama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhiimaparaakramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhīmaparākramā</i> au terrible héroïsme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiimaratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīmaratha</i> [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhiimaratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Bhīmaratha «au char terrible», fils de <a href="#entry-Uketumaan" class="skt-link">Ketumān</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Udivodaasa" class="skt-link">Divodāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiimasena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīmasena</i> [<a href="#entry-senaa" class="skt-link">senā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhiimasena" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Bhīmasena «à l'arme terrible» alias <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a>, second des 5 <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>, fils du dieu <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ukuntii" class="skt-link">Kuntī</a>; doué d'une force prodigieuse, il cassa l'arc de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> [<a href="#entry-zivadhanus" class="skt-link">śivadhanus</a>]; il possédait la conque <a href="#entry-Upau_n_dra" class="skt-link">Pauṇḍra</a>; il tua les <a href="#entry-raak_sasaas" class="skt-link">rākṣasās</a> <a href="#entry-Ubaka_1" class="skt-link">Baka_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukirmiira" class="skt-link">Kirmīra</a> et <a href="#entry-Uhi_dimba" class="skt-link">Hiḍimba</a>; il s'unit à la sœur <a href="#entry-Uhi_dimbaa" class="skt-link">Hiḍimbā</a> de ce dernier, qui enfanta du monstre <a href="#entry-Ugha_totkaca" class="skt-link">Ghaṭotkaca</a>; lors de l'exil des <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a> à la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a>, il se cacha comme le cuisinier <a href="#entry-Uballava" class="skt-link">Ballava</a>; il massacra le général <a href="#entry-Ukiicaka" class="skt-link">Kīcaka</a> pour défendre l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Udraupadii" class="skt-link">Draupadī</a>; lors de la grande bataille il tua <a href="#entry-Udu_hzaasana" class="skt-link">Duḥśāsana</a>, dont il avait juré de boire le sang; à la fin de la bataille, il combattit <a href="#entry-Uduryodhana" class="skt-link">Duryodhana</a> en combat singulier à la massue et lui brisa la cuisse, réalisant son vœu de venger l'insulte faite à <a href="#entry-Udraupadii" class="skt-link">Draupadī</a>, mais provoquant la colère de <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> par ce coup déloyal, indigne d'un <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a>; lorsque <a href="#entry-Udharmaraaja" class="skt-link">Dharmarāja</a> décida leur renoncement [<a href="#entry-tyaaga" class="skt-link">tyāga</a>], il suivit ses frères dans leur marche vers le Ciel, mais mourut en chemin, son <a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a> n'étant pas parfait, car il était glouton [<a href="#entry-Uv_rkodara" class="skt-link">Vṛkodara</a>] et égoïste; cf. <a href="#entry-Uballava" class="skt-link">Ballava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiimazafkara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīmaśaṅkara</i> [<a href="#entry-zafkara" class="skt-link">śaṅkara</a>] <a href="#entry-bhiimazafkara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Bhīmaśaṅkara, l'un des 12 signes radieux de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> [<a href="#entry-jyotirlifga" class="skt-link">jyotirliṅga</a>], établi près de Pune au <a href="#entry-Umahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Mahārāṣṭra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiimezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhīmeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-bhiimezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du <a href="#entry-zivalifga" class="skt-link">śivaliṅga</a> Bhīmeśvara, établi au <a href="#entry-raamak_setra" class="skt-link">rāmakṣetra</a> <a href="#entry-Udraak_saaraama" class="skt-link">Drākṣārāma</a> par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhiima" class="skt-link">bhīma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiiru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भीरु</span> <i>bhīru</i> [<a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a><a href="#entry--ru" class="skt-link">-ru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhiiru" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhiiru" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhiiruu" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhīrū</i> timide, peureux, craintif; lâche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui craint, intimidé par ⟨abl. iic.⟩ — voc. <i>bhīru</i> <a href="#entry-bhiiru" class="skt-link">f.</a> adr. Mademoiselle (façon gracieuse d'aborder une jeune femme).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भीत</span> <i>bhīta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhiita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhiita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhiitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhītā</i> effrayé, terrifié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भीति</span> <i>bhīti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhii_1" class="skt-link">bhī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhiiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> peur, crainte, effroi.<br> <i>bhītiḥ</i> <i>māstu</i> n'aie pas peur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhiititas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhītitas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. de crainte.<br> <a href="#entry-bhiiti" class="skt-link">bhīti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhik_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिक्ष्</span> <i>bhikṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-bhaj" class="skt-link">bhaj</a>] v. pr. md. <i>(bhikṣate)</i> pft. <i>(bibhikṣe)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhikṣita</i><i>)</i> demander qqc. ⟨acc. g.⟩ à qqn. ⟨acc. abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mendier, quêter, demander l'aumône.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिक्षा</span> <i>bhikṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhik_s" class="skt-link">bhikṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mendicité; aumône.<br> <i>bhikṣāṃ</i> <i>car</i> demander l'aumône; mendier.<br> <i>kṛṣito</i> <i>nāsti</i> <i>bhikṣām</i> Qui travaille la terre n'a pas besoin de mendier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhik_saa_tana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhikṣāṭana</i> [<a href="#entry-a_tana" class="skt-link">aṭana</a>] <a href="#entry-bhik_saa_tana" class="skt-link">n.</a> errance de mendicité — <a href="#entry-bhik_saa_tana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhikṣāṭana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «qui erre en Mendiant»; nu, il mendie sa nourriture dans une calotte crânienne; cf. <a href="#entry-Udaarukavana" class="skt-link">Dārukavana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhik_saartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhikṣārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhik_saartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> mendicité; aumône.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhik_saarthin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhikṣārthin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhik_saarthin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhik_saarthin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhik_saarthinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhikṣārthinī</i> mendiant.<br> <a href="#entry-bhik_saartha" class="skt-link">bhikṣārtha</a> <br> <a href="#entry-bhik_saa" class="skt-link">bhikṣā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhik_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिक्षु</span> <i>bhikṣu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhik_s" class="skt-link">bhikṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhik_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> mendiant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. homme au 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ stade de la vie brâhmanique [<a href="#entry-aazrama" class="skt-link">āśrama</a>], mendiant errant, ascétique; le <a href="#entry-puru_saartha" class="skt-link">puruṣārtha</a> associé est <a href="#entry-mok_sa" class="skt-link">mokṣa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-sannyaasin" class="skt-link">sannyāsin</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. moine bouddhiste, vivant d'aumônes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>bhikkhu</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhik_su_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhikṣuṇī</i> [<a href="#entry--nii" class="skt-link">-nī</a>] <a href="#entry-bhik_su_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. nonne buddhiste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhik_su_niisafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhikṣuṇīsaṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-safga" class="skt-link">saṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-bhik_su_niisafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. congrégation des nonnes bouddhistes; elle est éteinte aujourd'hui.<br> <a href="#entry-bhik_su" class="skt-link">bhikṣu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhik_suka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhikṣuka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-bhik_suka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. mendiant errant; not. brâhmane d'un ordre mendiant — <a href="#entry-bhik_sukii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhikṣukī</i> soc. mendiante <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. nonne buddhiste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhilla">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिल्ल</span> <i>bhilla</i> <a href="#entry-bhilla" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. nom d'une race tribale montagneuse des monts <a href="#entry-Uvindhya" class="skt-link">Vindhya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. hors-caste; fils d'un <a href="#entry-zabara" class="skt-link">śabara</a> et d'une <a href="#entry-andhrii" class="skt-link">andhrī</a> — <a href="#entry-bhillii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhillī</i> femme de race <a href="#entry-bhilla" class="skt-link">bhilla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhindat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिन्दत्</span> <i>bhindat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhindat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhindat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhindatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhindatī</i> qui casse, qui perce, qui détruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhindipaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिन्दिपाल</span> <i>bhindipāla</i> [<a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>-<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-bhindipaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> javelot <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> catapulte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhinna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भिन्न</span> <i>bhinna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhinna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhinna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhinnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhinnā</i> fendu, brisé, fracturé, détruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> transgressé, violé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> divisé en parties; éclaté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perturbé, interrompu; changé, altéré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> détaché; libre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> séparé, différencié; séduit, corrompu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> distinct, différent de ⟨iic. abl⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mélangé avec ⟨iic. i.⟩ — <a href="#entry-bhinna" class="skt-link">m.</a> math. fraction — <a href="#entry-bhinna" class="skt-link">n.</a> fragment, morceau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blessure à l'arme blanche, coup de poignard — <a href="#entry-bhinnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhinnā</i> natu. bot. <i>Sansevieria roxburghiana</i>, sorte d'agave; cf. <a href="#entry-muurvaa" class="skt-link">mūrvā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhinnaka_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhinnakaṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_ta" class="skt-link">kaṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhinnaka_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant en rut [«dont les tempes ont éclaté»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhinnakara_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhinnakaraṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_ta" class="skt-link">karaṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhinnakara_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhinnakariindrakumbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhinnakarīndrakumbha</i> [<a href="#entry-kariindrakumbha" class="skt-link">karīndrakumbha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhinnakariindrakumbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant de combat en rut.<br> <i>bhinnakarīndrakumbhamuktāmaya</i> fait des perles tombant des bosses frontales des éléphants de guerre en rut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhinnaruci">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhinnaruci</i> [<a href="#entry-ruci" class="skt-link">ruci</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhinnaruci" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhinnaruci" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhinnaruci" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a un goût différent.<br> <i>bhinnarucirhi</i> <i>lokaḥ</i> [Ragh.] Tous les goûts sont dans la nature.<br> <a href="#entry-bhinna" class="skt-link">bhinna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhitti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भित्ति</span> <i>bhitti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhitti" class="skt-link">f.</a> [séparation] mur, muraille, paroi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhitticcheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhitticcheda</i> [<a href="#entry-cheda" class="skt-link">cheda</a>] <a href="#entry-bhitticcheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> brèche dans un mur.<br> <a href="#entry-bhitti" class="skt-link">bhitti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bho">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भो</span> <i>bho</i> cf. <a href="#entry-bhos" class="skt-link">bhos</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bho_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोट</span> <i>bhoṭa</i> <a href="#entry-bho_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Bhoṭa, le Tibet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. Bhoutan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhoga_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोग</span> <i>bhoga_1</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuj_1" class="skt-link">bhuj_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhoga_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> anneau (d'un serpent).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhoga_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोग</span> <i>bhoga_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] <a href="#entry-bhoga_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> consommation; nourriture; repas, banquet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plaisir, richesse, jouissance; perception, sentiment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plaisir sexuel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> profit, gain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. fruit (d'une action), récompense.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhogalaabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhogalābha</i> [<a href="#entry-laabha" class="skt-link">lābha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhogalaabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. avantage obtenu par un créditeur par le rendement d'un bien prêté en gage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhogavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhogavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] <a href="#entry-bhogavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhogavatī</i> myth. np. de la rivière Bhogavatī ou Gange souterrain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. capitale souterraine des dragons [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-bhoga_1" class="skt-link">bhoga_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhogin_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोगिन्</span> <i>bhogin_1</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuj_1" class="skt-link">bhuj_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhogin_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhoginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhoginī</i> [«qui a des anneaux»] serpent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhogin_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोगिन्</span> <i>bhogin_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhogin_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhogin_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhoginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhoginī</i> jouisseur — <a href="#entry-bhoginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhoginī</i> soc. favorite d'un prince.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhogopabhogavrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhogopabhogavrata</i> [<a href="#entry-upabhoga" class="skt-link">upabhoga</a>-<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] <a href="#entry-bhogopabhogavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> jn. l'observance de consommation limitée, un vœu méritoire [<a href="#entry-gu_navrata" class="skt-link">guṇavrata</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-bhoga_2" class="skt-link">bhoga_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhogya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोग्य</span> <i>bhogya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhogya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhogya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhogyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhogyā</i> dont on doit jouir; digne qu'on en jouisse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dont on peut se nourrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhoj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोज्</span> <i>bhoj</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(bhojayati)</i> nourrir; réjouir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhoja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोज</span> <i>bhoja</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhoja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhoja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhojaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhojā</i> qui donne le plaisir; libéral <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> joyeux, bon vivant, jouisseur — <a href="#entry-bhoja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-yaadava" class="skt-link">yādava</a> Bhoja «Épicurien», roi des <a href="#entry-bhojaas" class="skt-link">bhojās</a>, descendant de <a href="#entry-Uv_r_s_ni" class="skt-link">Vṛṣṇi</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Uh_rdiika" class="skt-link">Hṛdīka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_rtavarmaa" class="skt-link">Kṛtavarmā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du roi Bhoja_1 du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhojadeva" class="skt-link">Bhojadeva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>bhojās</i> géo. nom d'un pays et de ses habitants, de capitale <a href="#entry-Ubhojapurii" class="skt-link">Bhojapurī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uuziinaraas" class="skt-link">Uśīnarās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhojadeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-bhojadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a> Bhojadeva, fils aîné de <a href="#entry-Usindhuraaja" class="skt-link">Sindhurāja</a>; sa capitale était <a href="#entry-Udhaaraa" class="skt-link">Dhārā</a>; il régna de 1018 à 1054; il repoussa les <a href="#entry-Uturu_skaas" class="skt-link">Turuṣkās</a> à <a href="#entry-Udvaarakaa" class="skt-link">Dvārakā</a>; il combattit, et fut finalement vaincu par les <a href="#entry-Ucaalukyaas" class="skt-link">Cālukyās</a>; il était lettré, avait à sa cour de nombreux poètes, et organisait des tournois de poésie; le <a href="#entry-Ubhojaprabandha" class="skt-link">Bhojaprabandha</a> en fit la légende dorée; on lui attribue de très nombreux ouvrages, not. des traités sur le style poétique en sanskrit (<a href="#entry-Usarasvatiika_n_thaabhara_na_1" class="skt-link">Sarasvatīkaṇṭhābharaṇa_1</a>), sur la poésie et la dramaturgie (<a href="#entry-Uz_rfgaaraprakaaza" class="skt-link">Śṛṅgāraprakāśa</a>), sur l'hygiène (<a href="#entry-Ucaarucaryaa" class="skt-link">Cārucaryā</a>); son <a href="#entry-Uraajamaarta_n_da" class="skt-link">Rājamārtaṇḍa</a> est un commentaire du <a href="#entry-Uyogasuutra" class="skt-link">Yogasūtra</a>; son <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_nacampuu" class="skt-link">Rāmāyaṇacampū</a> est un poème classique du sanskrit médiéval; enfin on lui doit la grammaire du sanskrit appelée elle aussi <a href="#entry-Usarasvatiika_n_thaabhara_na_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatīkaṇṭhābharaṇa_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोजन</span> <i>bhojana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] <a href="#entry-bhojana" class="skt-link">n.</a> nourriture, aliment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> alimentation, repas — <a href="#entry-bhojanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhojanā</i> ifc. nourri de; qui sert de nourriture à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojanapaatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhojanapātra</i> [<a href="#entry-paatra" class="skt-link">pātra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhojanapaatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> récipient alimentaire; bol.<br> <a href="#entry-bhojana" class="skt-link">bhojana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojaprabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhojaprabandha</i> [<a href="#entry-prabandha" class="skt-link">prabandha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhojaprabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Bhojaprabandha «Geste du roi <a href="#entry-Ubhoja_1" class="skt-link">Bhoja_1</a>» de <a href="#entry-Uvallaala" class="skt-link">Vallāla</a>; c'est une fiction qui met en scène de nombreux poètes (souvent non contemporains) à la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Ubhojadeva" class="skt-link">Bhojadeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhojapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-bhojapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Bhojapura, dans le <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>, près de mod. Paṭnā — <a href="#entry-bhojapurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhojapurī</i> géo. np. de Bhojapurī, id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojaraajan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhojarājan</i> [<a href="#entry-raajan" class="skt-link">rājan</a>] <a href="#entry-bhojaraajan" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Bhojarājā; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhojadeva" class="skt-link">Bhojadeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhojavṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-bhojav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. du Bhojavṛtti, commentaire sur le <a href="#entry-Uyogasuutra" class="skt-link">Yogasūtra</a> de <a href="#entry-Upata_njali_2" class="skt-link">Patañjali_2</a> [<a href="#entry-Upaata_njalav_rtti" class="skt-link">Pātañjalavṛtti</a>] dû au roi <a href="#entry-Ubhojadeva" class="skt-link">Bhojadeva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uraajamaarta_n_da" class="skt-link">Rājamārtaṇḍa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojazaalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhojaśālā</i> [<a href="#entry-zaalaa" class="skt-link">śālā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhojazaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> hist. np. de la Bhojaśālā «Bibliothèque du roi <a href="#entry-Ubhoja_1" class="skt-link">Bhoja_1</a>», école de sanskrit fondée par le roi <a href="#entry-Ubhojadeva" class="skt-link">Bhojadeva</a> à <a href="#entry-Udhaaraa" class="skt-link">Dhārā</a>, dans le temple de <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī</a>; elle fut pillée en 1140 par <a href="#entry-Usiddharaaja" class="skt-link">Siddharāja</a>, qui ramena en butin ses livres, et not. la grammaire <a href="#entry-Usarasvatiika_n_thaabhara_na_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatīkaṇṭhābharaṇa_2</a>; elle fut alors transformée en mosquée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोजिन्</span> <i>bhojin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-bhojin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhojin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhojinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhojinī</i> qui mange; qui prend plaisir à, qui jouit de.<br> <i>śīghrabhojin</i> qui mange vite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojo_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhojoṣṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-u_s_na" class="skt-link">uṣṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhojo_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhojo_s_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhojo_s_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhojoṣṇā</i> trop chaud pour être mangé.<br> <a href="#entry-bhoja" class="skt-link">bhoja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोज्य</span> <i>bhojya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhojya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhojya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhojyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhojyā</i> dont on peut jouir, à consommer; comestible; que l'on doit nourrir; dont la nourriture peut être mangée par ⟨g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dont on peut ou doit se servir — <a href="#entry-bhojya" class="skt-link">n.</a> nourriture, aliment, repas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhojyaanna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhojyānna</i> [<a href="#entry-anna" class="skt-link">anna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhojyaanna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhojyaanna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhojyaannaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhojyānnā</i> soc. convenable à préparer le repas (se dit d'un <a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a> propre à préparer la nourriture d'un <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-bhojya" class="skt-link">bhojya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhoktavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोक्तव्य</span> <i>bhoktavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhoktavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhoktavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhoktavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhoktavyā</i> dont on peut jouir, que l'on peut manger; à utiliser.<br> <i>bhoktavyam</i> il faut manger.<br> <i>asti</i> <i>bhoktavyam</i> il y a à manger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhoktu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोक्तु</span> <i>bhoktu</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhoktu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhoktu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhoktu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui profite; qui mange; qui ressent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui gouverne, qui utilise — <a href="#entry-bhoktu" class="skt-link">m.</a> utilisateur; possédant; mari; prince <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhos">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भोस्</span> <i>bhos</i> var. <i>bho</i> [<a href="#entry-bhavat_2" class="skt-link">bhavat_2</a>] interj. d'interpellation respectueuse: monsieur! <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ô! ⟨voc.⟩.<br> <i>bho</i> <i>bhos</i> holà! oyez, oyez!<br> <i>bho</i> <i>rāma</i> ô <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>!
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhra_mz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रंश्</span> <i>bhraṃś</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(bhraṃśate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(bhraśyati)</i> pr. md. <i>(bhraśyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhraṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> tomber, tomber en morceaux, tomber de ⟨abl.⟩; déchoir; disparaître — ca. <i>(bhraṃśayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhraṃśita</i><i>)</i> faire tomber ou déchoir, causer la perte de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhra_mza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रंश</span> <i>bhraṃśa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhra_mz" class="skt-link">bhraṃś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhra_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhra_mza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhra_mzaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhraṃśā</i> qui tombe; qui se détache de ⟨abl. iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-bhra_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de tomber en se détachant; perte, disparition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> séparation; abandon; fait d'être privé de ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhra_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रष्ट</span> <i>bhraṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhra_mz" class="skt-link">bhraṃś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhra_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhra_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhra_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhraṣṭā</i> tombé, déchu (de ⟨abl. iic.⟩); corrompu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> disparu; en ruines.<br> <i>yogabhraṣṭa</i> phil. ayant perdu la grâce yogique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhra_s_tazrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhraṣṭaśrī</i> [<a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhra_s_tazrii" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhra_s_tazrii" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhra_s_tazrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> ruiné.<br> <a href="#entry-bhra_s_ta" class="skt-link">bhraṣṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhraaj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्राज्</span> <i>bhrāj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(bhrājate)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> briller, rayonner, étinceler — ca. <i>(bhrājayati)</i> faire briller, illuminer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. φλεγω; lat. <i>flagro</i>; ang. bright; fr. flamme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhraama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्राम</span> <i>bhrāma</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhrama" class="skt-link">bhrama</a>] <a href="#entry-bhraama" class="skt-link">m.</a> égarement, hésitation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhraamara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रामर</span> <i>bhrāmara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-bhramara" class="skt-link">bhramara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhraamara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhraamara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhraamarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhrāmarī</i> concernant l'abeille — <a href="#entry-bhraamara" class="skt-link">n.</a> miel — <a href="#entry-bhraamarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhrāmarī</i> phil. [tantr.] np. de Bhrāmarī «Bourdonnante», l'une des 8 puissances divines [<a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhraanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रान्त</span> <i>bhrānta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhram" class="skt-link">bhram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhraanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhraantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhrāntā</i> qui erre; égaré, troublé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> parcouru — <a href="#entry-bhraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> errance, tribulation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhraanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रान्ति</span> <i>bhrānti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhram" class="skt-link">bhram</a>] <a href="#entry-bhraanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> errement, divaguation; égarement; hésitation, doute, perplexité; trouble, confusion; illusion; erreur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de rouler (roue); vibration; tremblement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhraat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रातृ</span> <i>bhrātṛ</i> <a href="#entry-bhraat_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> frère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mon frère (ami intime) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>bhrātarau</i> frère et sœur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. φρατηρ; lat. <i>frater</i>; ang. brother; all. Bruder; fr. frère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhraat_rjaayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhrātṛjāyā</i> [<a href="#entry-jaayaa" class="skt-link">jāyā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhraat_rjaayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> belle-sœur (femme d'un frère).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhraat_rtva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhrātṛtva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-bhraat_rtva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fraternité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhraatriiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhrātrīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhraatriiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> neveu (fils d'un frère) — <a href="#entry-bhraatriiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhrātrīyā</i> nièce.<br> <a href="#entry-bhraat_r" class="skt-link">bhrātṛ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रम्</span> <i>bhram</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(bhramati)</i> v.
