[<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(bhrāmyati)</i> pft. <i>(babhrāma)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhrānta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> pérégriner; errer, divaguer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tituber; se déplacer irrégulièrement; circuler (étoiles, planètes) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se répandre (nouvelles) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> douter, hésiter; commettre une erreur — ca. <i>(bhrāmayati)</i> agiter, brandir.<br> <i>bhikṣām</i> <i>bhram</i> errer en mendiant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रम</span> <i>bhrama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhram" class="skt-link">bhram</a>] <a href="#entry-bhrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> divagation, errance; erreur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> déplacement, rotation; trajectoire; roulement (yeux); tourbillon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> source, fontaine, cours d'eau — <a href="#entry-bhramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhramā</i> ifc. id.<br> <i>bhramam</i> <i>dā</i> se balancer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhrama_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रमण</span> <i>bhramaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhram" class="skt-link">bhram</a>] <a href="#entry-bhrama_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> pérégrination; errance; circumambulation autour de ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> divagation; révolution, orbite (planète) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vertige; étourdissement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de commettre une erreur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhramaatmaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhramātmaka</i> [<a href="#entry-aatmaka" class="skt-link">ātmaka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhramaatmaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhramaatmaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhramaatmika" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhramātmika</i> phil. caractérise la connaissance [<a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a>] erronée; opp. <a href="#entry-pramaatmaka" class="skt-link">pramātmaka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhrama" class="skt-link">bhrama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhramara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रमर</span> <i>bhramara</i> [<a href="#entry-bhrama" class="skt-link">bhrama</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhramara" class="skt-link">m.</a> abeille; insecte volant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (poésie) amant [«butineur»] — <a href="#entry-bhramaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhramarā</i> ifc. abeille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plante grimpante — <a href="#entry-bhramarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhramarī</i> abeille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhruku_tii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रुकुटी</span> <i>bhrukuṭī</i> [<a href="#entry-bhruu" class="skt-link">bhrū</a>-<a href="#entry-ku_t" class="skt-link">kuṭ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhruku_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> froncement de sourcils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhruku_tiimukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhrukuṭīmukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhruku_tiimukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhruku_tiimukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhruku_tiimukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhrukuṭīmukhī</i> qui fronce les sourcils — <a href="#entry-bhruku_tiimukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> visage courroucé ou renfrogné.<br> <a href="#entry-bhruku_tii" class="skt-link">bhrukuṭī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhruu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रू</span> <i>bhrū</i> <a href="#entry-bhruu" class="skt-link">f.</a> sourcil; cf. <a href="#entry-subhru" class="skt-link">subhru</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. οφρυς; ang. brow; all. braue; fr. froncer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhruu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भ्रूण</span> <i>bhrūṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_r" class="skt-link">bhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhruu_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> foetus — <a href="#entry-bhruu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> enfant, garçon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> brâhmane érudit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhruu_naghna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhrūṇaghna</i> [<a href="#entry-ghna" class="skt-link">ghna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhruu_naghna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhruu_naghna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhruu_naghnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhrūṇaghnā</i> avorteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> meurtrier d'un brâhmane érudit.<br> <a href="#entry-bhruu_na" class="skt-link">bhrūṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhruuku_tii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhrūkuṭī</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_tii" class="skt-link">kuṭī</a>] <a href="#entry-bhruuku_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> froncement des sourcils.<br> <i>bhrūkuṭīṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> froncer les sourcils.<br> <i>bhrūkuṭīṃ</i> <i>bandh</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhruumadhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhrūmadhya</i> [<a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">madhya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhruumadhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> espace entre les sourcils.<br> <a href="#entry-bhruu" class="skt-link">bhrū</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भु</span> <i>bhu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuu_2" class="skt-link">bhū_2</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-bhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui devient, qui est; produit par.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhugna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुग्न</span> <i>bhugna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhuj_1" class="skt-link">bhuj_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhugna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhugna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhugnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhugnā</i> courbé, tordu; froncé (sourcils).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuj_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुज्</span> <i>bhuj_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(bhujati)</i> pft. <i>(bubhoja)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhugna</i><i>)</i> courber, ployer, plier, fléchir, tordre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. φευγω; lat. <i>fugio</i>; all. biegen.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuj_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुज्</span> <i>bhuj_2</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(bhunakti)</i> pr. md. <i>(bhuṅkte)</i> pft. <i>(bubhoja)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhukta</i><i>)</i> ppr. md. <i>(bhuñjāna)</i> inf. <i>(bhoktum)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhoktavya</i>, <i>bhogya</i>, <i>bhojya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>upa</i><i>)</i> jouir de, posséder ⟨acc.⟩; jouir charnellement de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manger, goûter; boire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> utiliser, employer, exploiter; gouverner, régir — ca. <i>(bhojayati)</i> ca. <i>(bhuñjāpayati)</i> nourrir; nourrir qqn. ⟨acc.⟩ avec qqc. ⟨acc. i.⟩ — dés. <i>(bubhukṣati)</i> désirer manger ou posséder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>fungor</i>, <i>fructus</i>; fr. fruit.<br> <i>bhavān</i> <i>bhunaktu</i> c'est le moment de vous nourrir.<br> <i>bhojaṃ</i> <i>bhojaṃ</i> <i>vrajati</i> il marche après avoir mangé plusieurs fois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuj_3">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुज्</span> <i>bhuj_3</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-bhuj_3" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuj_3" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuj_3" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui jouit de, qui aime, qui mange, qui gouverne, qui régit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुज</span> <i>bhuja</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuj_1" class="skt-link">bhuj_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> bras; trompe d'éléphant; tronc d'arbre — <a href="#entry-bhujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhujā</i> courbe, repli; anneau de serpent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. côté d'une figure géométrique.<br> <i>bhujais</i> <i>tṝ</i> traverser à la nage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhujafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhujaṃga</i> var. <i>bhujaṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuja_mga" class="skt-link">m.</a> serpent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> compagnon de débauche; compagnon dissolu d'un prince; amant; souteneur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du génie-serpent [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>] Bhujaṅga <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 8.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhujafgama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhujaṃgama</i> [<a href="#entry-gama" class="skt-link">gama</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuja_mgama" class="skt-link">m.</a> serpent.<br> <a href="#entry-bhujam" class="skt-link">bhujam</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhujaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhujaga</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhujaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> serpent [«qui serpente»] — <a href="#entry-bhujagii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhujagī</i> serpent femelle.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuja" class="skt-link">bhuja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhujam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुजम्</span> <i>bhujam</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-bhuja" class="skt-link">bhuja</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuji_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुजिष्य</span> <i>bhujiṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuji_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> serviteur — <a href="#entry-bhuji_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhujiṣyā</i> servante.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhujyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुज्यु</span> <i>bhujyu</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhujyu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhujyu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhujyu" class="skt-link">f.</a> riche, opulent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. [RV.] np. du prince Bhujyu, «l'Opulent», fils du roi <a href="#entry-Utugra" class="skt-link">Tugra</a>; [<a href="#entry-Usaaya_na" class="skt-link">Sāyaṇa</a>] son père l'envoya sur mer pour attaquer ses ennemis réfugiés dans une île lointaine; ayant fait naufrage au large, il implora les <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a> qui le sauvèrent de la noyade.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुक्त</span> <i>bhukta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhuktā</i> dont on a joui ou usé; possédé; mangé — ifc. qui se nourrit de — <a href="#entry-bhukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> nourriture, repas; jouissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhukti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुक्ति</span> <i>bhukti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] <a href="#entry-bhukti" class="skt-link">f.</a> profit, possession, consommation; plaisir, jouissance, usufruit; victuailles <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. attachement aux plaisirs profanes; opp. <a href="#entry-mukti" class="skt-link">mukti</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. trajet d'un objet céleste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuu_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूष्</span> <i>bhūṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(bhūṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhūṣita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> faire des efforts pour ⟨dat.⟩, s'efforcer d'obtenir — ca. <i>(bhūṣayati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> orner, parer, décorer; embellir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuu_s_nu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूष्णु</span> <i>bhūṣṇu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhuu_s_nu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuu_s_nu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuu_s_nu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui profite, qui croît; prospère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> désireux de prospérité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuu_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूषण</span> <i>bhūṣaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuu_s" class="skt-link">bhūṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhūṣaṇā</i> ifc. qui orne, qui décore — <a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> — <a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> ornement, parure, décor <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. commentaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuu_sa_nabha_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūṣaṇabhaṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_nabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Bhūṣaṇa <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Ubaa_na_2" class="skt-link">Bāṇa_2</a>; après la mort de son père, il compléta la <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a> suivant ses plans.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuu_sa_nayojana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūṣaṇayojana</i> [<a href="#entry-yojana" class="skt-link">yojana</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_nayojana" class="skt-link">n.</a> l'art de la parure, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">bhūṣaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuu_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूषा</span> <i>bhūṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuu_s" class="skt-link">bhūṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuu_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> ornement, décoration.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuu_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूषिन्</span> <i>bhūṣin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-bhuu_s" class="skt-link">bhūṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhuu_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuu_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuu_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhūṣiṇī</i> ifc. orné de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuu_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">भू</span> <i>bhū_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(bhavati)</i> pr. md. <i>(bhavate)</i> imp. <i>(bhava)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>bhaviṣyati</i><i>)</i> fut. péri. <i>(bhavitā)</i> pft. <i>(babhūva)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <i>(abhūt)</i> cond. <i>(abhaviṣyat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhūta</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(bhavat_1)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhavanīya</i>, <i>bhavitavya</i>, <i>bhavya</i>, <i>bhāvya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>bhūtvā</i>, <i>-bhūya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>abhi</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>tiras</i>, <i>parā</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>bahis</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> se produire, avoir lieu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> devenir, naître <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être, exister, se trouver (être en devenir, opp. <a href="#entry-as_1" class="skt-link">as_1</a> être réalisé) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> appartenir à — ca. <i>(bhāvayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>bhāvita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>bhāvya</i><i>)</i> faire se manifester; produire, créer, causer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> encourager, réconforter; reconnaître, honorer — ps. <i>(bhūyate)</i> être créé — int. <i>(bobhoti)</i> int. <i>(bobhavīti)</i> int. md. <i>(bobhūyate)</i> être fréquemment, avoir l'habitude de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être transformé en ⟨acc.⟩ — dés. <i>(bubhūṣati)</i> dés. md. <i>(bubhūṣate)</i> vouloir devenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. εφυν; lat. <i>fui</i>; ang. to be; all. bin; fr. physique.<br> <i>bhavatu</i> adv. soit! eh bien! marché conclu (marque une résolution prise).<br> <i>abhaviṣyat</i> s'il y a.<br> <i>evaṃ</i> <i>bhavatu</i> qu'il en soit ainsi.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>bhāvayet</i> [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] que faut-il accomplir?<br> <i>kena</i> <i>bhāvayet</i> par quel moyen peut-on y arriver?<br> <i>kathaṃ</i> <i>bhāvayet</i> comment faut-il s'y prendre?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuu_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भू</span> <i>bhū_2</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuu_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> la Terre; sol, terrain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> région, endroit, lieu, emplacement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de devenir ou d'apparaître — ind. <i>bhūr</i> [<i>bhūrloka</i>] myth. np. de Bhū la Terre, premier monde céleste [<a href="#entry-loka" class="skt-link">loka</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-lokatraya" class="skt-link">lokatraya</a>, <a href="#entry-vyaah_rti" class="skt-link">vyāhṛti</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>bhuvas</i> les mondes; voc. cf. <a href="#entry-bhuvas" class="skt-link">bhuvas</a> — loc. <i>bhuvi</i> adv. sur Terre — <a href="#entry-bhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhu" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhu</i> ifc. qui est, qui devient, produit par.<br> <i>bhūr</i> <i>bhuvar</i> <i>svar</i> par la terre, les mondes et le ciel (exclamation rituelle [<a href="#entry-vyaah_rti" class="skt-link">vyāhṛti</a>]); cf. <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a>.<br> <i>rahobhuvi</i> adv. en un lieu secret.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuubh_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūbhṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-bh_rt" class="skt-link">bhṛt</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuubh_rt" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> montagne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuubhuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūbhuj</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuj_3" class="skt-link">bhuj_3</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuubhuj" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi, prince [«qui régit la terre»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूम</span> <i>bhūma</i> ifc. de <a href="#entry-bhuuman" class="skt-link">bhūman</a>.<br> <i>dhruvāya</i> <i>bhūmāya</i> <i>namaḥ</i> Hommage à la Terre éternelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूमन्</span> <i>bhūman</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuu_2" class="skt-link">bhū_2</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuuman" class="skt-link">n.</a> la Terre, le Monde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> territoire, sol, terrain; pays, contrée — <a href="#entry-bhuuman" class="skt-link">m.</a> plénitude, abondance; richesse; multitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] l'infini <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. pluriel — ifc. rempli de.<br> <i>bhūmnā</i> adv. généralement, en général.<br> <i>bhūmni</i> adv. gram. au pluriel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूमि</span> <i>bhūmi</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuu_2" class="skt-link">bhū_2</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">f.</a> terre, sol, terrain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lieu, place, terroir; emplacement, terrain; position, poste, situation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Bhūmi la Terre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rôle, personnage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 1 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la section [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-Ubhuumi" class="skt-link">Bhūmi</a> d'un <a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a> traitant de mythes se passant sur Terre — ifc. objet propre à, réceptacle de.<br> <i>bhūmau</i> adv. par terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumicchidra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmicchidra</i> [<a href="#entry-chidra" class="skt-link">chidra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumicchidra" class="skt-link">n.</a> défrichage, première culture de la terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumicchidranyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmicchidranyāya</i> [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumicchidranyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. droit du sol à celui qui le cultive en premier.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuumicchidra" class="skt-link">bhūmicchidra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumig_rha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmigṛha</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rha" class="skt-link">gṛha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumig_rha" class="skt-link">n.</a> chambre souterraine; cachot; caveau — a. <a href="#entry-bhuumig_rha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuumig_rha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuumig_rhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhūmigṛhā</i> [«dont la terre est la résidence»] mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumihaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmihāra</i> [<a href="#entry-haara_1" class="skt-link">hāra_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumihaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. sous-classe de brâhmanes agriculteurs des plaines indo-gangétiques; ils font des dons, mais ne quêtent pas, et n'officient pas comme prêtres.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">bhūmi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmija</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhuumija" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuumija" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuumijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhūmijā</i> issu de la terre; né du sol — <a href="#entry-bhuumija" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Bhūmija «né de la Terre», un nom de Mars; cf. <a href="#entry-Uafgaaraka" class="skt-link">Aṅgāraka</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukuja" class="skt-link">Kuja</a>, <a href="#entry-Umafgala" class="skt-link">Maṅgala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूमिका</span> <i>bhūmikā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">bhūmi</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> terre, sol <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> préface, introduction d'un ouvrage; (théâtre) liste des rôles des acteurs — ifc. emplacement de; sol en; plancher, étage; niveau.<br> <i>bhūmikāyām</i> adv. ifc. dans le rôle de ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <i>lakṣmībhūmikāyāṃ</i> <i>vartamānā</i> jouant le rôle de Lak.smii.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumim">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूमिम्</span> <i>bhūmim</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">bhūmi</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmipa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_2" class="skt-link">pa_2</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«protecteur de la terre»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumipaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmipāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumipaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«protecteur de la terre»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> thai Bhumibol.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumipati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmipati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumipati" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«maître (ou époux) de la terre»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumipuujaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmipūjā</i> [<a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumipuujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. rite de consécration du terrain d'un édifice, not. d'un temple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumisparza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmisparśa</i> [<a href="#entry-sparza" class="skt-link">sparśa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumisparza" class="skt-link">m.</a> toucher de la terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumisparzamudraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmisparśamudrā</i> [<a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumisparzamudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. geste du «toucher de la terre», par lequel <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> prit la Terre à témoin de ses mérites au moment de son Éveil (en <a href="#entry-padmaasana" class="skt-link">padmāsana</a>, main droite sur le genou droit, doigts étendus touchant le sol).<br> <a href="#entry-bhuumisparza" class="skt-link">bhūmisparśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumisuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmisuta</i> [<a href="#entry-suta_2" class="skt-link">suta_2</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumisuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. de Bhūmisuta, épith. de <a href="#entry-Umafgala" class="skt-link">Maṅgala</a> «fils de la Terre».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuumi_njaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūmiṃjaya</i> [<a href="#entry-jaya" class="skt-link">jaya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuumi_mjaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Bhūmiñjaya «Conquérant de la Terre», titre du prince <a href="#entry-Uuttara" class="skt-link">Uttara</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuumim" class="skt-link">bhūmim</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuupaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūpāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuupaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«gardien de la Terre»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuupati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūpati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuupati" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«maître de la Terre»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuraala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūrāla</i> [<a href="#entry-raala" class="skt-link">rāla</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuuraala" class="skt-link">m.</a> lave.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuu_2" class="skt-link">bhū_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूरि</span> <i>bhūri</i> a. <a href="#entry-bhuuri" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuuri" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuuri" class="skt-link">f.</a> nombreux, abondant; grand, important.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuurizas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūriśas</i> [<a href="#entry--zas" class="skt-link">-śas</a>] adv. multiplement, en abondance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuurizravas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūriśravas</i> [<a href="#entry-zravas" class="skt-link">śravas</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuurizravas" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du prince Bhūriśravā «Tonitruant», fils de <a href="#entry-Usomadatta" class="skt-link">Somadatta</a>; il rallia le clan des <a href="#entry-kauravaas" class="skt-link">kauravās</a> comme général [<a href="#entry-ak_sauhi_niipati" class="skt-link">akṣauhiṇīpati</a>]; alors qu'il tenait son compatriote <a href="#entry-Uyuyudhaana" class="skt-link">Yuyudhāna</a> à sa merci, <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> lui coupa le bras d'une flèche, et <a href="#entry-Uyuyudhaana" class="skt-link">Yuyudhāna</a> le décapita.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuuri" class="skt-link">bhūri</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuurja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूर्ज</span> <i>bhūrja</i> <a href="#entry-bhuurja" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Botula bhojpatra</i>, bouleau; on écrit sur son écorce — <a href="#entry-bhuurja" class="skt-link">n.</a> feuille d'écorce de bouleau utilisée pour écrire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> document, contrat écrit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. birch.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuurjapattra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūrjapattra</i> [<a href="#entry-pattra" class="skt-link">pattra</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuurjapattra" class="skt-link">n.</a> écorce de bouleau servant à écrire.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuurja" class="skt-link">bhūrja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूत</span> <i>bhūta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhūtā</i> été; devenu, qui a existé, qui s'est produit; passé; vrai, réel — ifc. qui est devenu, qui est pareil à, qui consiste en — <a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> être vivant, âme, créature <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. esprit, fantôme, génie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] une substance [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] parmi les 5 éléments grossiers [<a href="#entry-pa_ncabhuutaani" class="skt-link">pañcabhūtāni</a>]: <a href="#entry-aakaaza" class="skt-link">ākāśa</a> l'Éther ou Espace, <a href="#entry-vaayu" class="skt-link">vāyu</a> l'Air, <a href="#entry-tejas" class="skt-link">tejas</a> le Feu, <a href="#entry-ap" class="skt-link">ap</a> l'Eau et <a href="#entry-p_rthivii" class="skt-link">pṛthivī</a> la Terre; ils dérivent des 5 perceptions [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-suuk_smabhuuta" class="skt-link">sūkṣmabhūta</a>, <a href="#entry-sthuulabhuuta" class="skt-link">sthūlabhūta</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 5 — <a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. esprit d'un défunt, spectre; les <i>bhūtās</i> sont une classe d'êtres maléfiques.<br> <i>...</i> <i>bhūta</i> étant ...
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutabhavyabhavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtabhavyabhavat</i> [<a href="#entry-bhavya" class="skt-link">bhavya</a>-<a href="#entry-bhavat_1" class="skt-link">bhavat_1</a>] iic. le passé, le futur et le présent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutabhavyabhavatprabhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtabhavyabhavatprabhu</i> [<a href="#entry-prabhu" class="skt-link">prabhu</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutabhavyabhavatprabhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. maître du passé, du futur et du présent; <a href="#entry-Unahu_sa" class="skt-link">Nahuṣa</a> s'en vantait.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuutabhavyabhavat" class="skt-link">bhūtabhavyabhavat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutadhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtadhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-dhaara_1" class="skt-link">dhāra_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhuutadhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuutadhaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuutadhaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhūtadhārā</i> qui garde à l'esprit, qui se souvient — <a href="#entry-bhuutadhaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhūtadhārā</i> la Terre [«base des éléments»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>bhūtadhāriṇī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtala</i> [<a href="#entry-taala" class="skt-link">tāla</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutala" class="skt-link">n.</a> sol; la Terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutalipi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtalipi</i> [<a href="#entry-lipi" class="skt-link">lipi</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutalipi" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] «alphabet des éléments», disposition particulière de l'alphabet sanskrit (sans voyelles longues) associée à l'évolution des éléments matériels.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutanaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtanātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutanaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhūtanātha, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Maître des créatures».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutasafkhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtasaṃkhya</i> [<a href="#entry-safkhya" class="skt-link">saṃkhya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutasa_mkhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> système de numération par analogie; par ex. <a href="#entry-abhra" class="skt-link">abhra</a>, <a href="#entry-ambara" class="skt-link">ambara</a>, <a href="#entry-kha" class="skt-link">kha</a>, <a href="#entry-viyat" class="skt-link">viyat</a>, <a href="#entry-vyoman" class="skt-link">vyoman</a> ou <a href="#entry-zuunya" class="skt-link">śūnya</a> pour 0, <a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">ādi</a>, <a href="#entry-indu" class="skt-link">indu</a>, <a href="#entry-ka_2" class="skt-link">ka_2</a>, <a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>, <a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">bhūmi</a> ou <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> pour 1, <a href="#entry-netra" class="skt-link">netra</a>, <a href="#entry-pha_niindra" class="skt-link">phaṇīndra</a>, <a href="#entry-yama_2" class="skt-link">yama_2</a> ou <a href="#entry-yamala" class="skt-link">yamala</a> pour 2, <a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a>, <a href="#entry-bhuvana" class="skt-link">bhuvana</a>, <a href="#entry-muurti" class="skt-link">mūrti</a> ou <a href="#entry-loka" class="skt-link">loka</a> pour 3, <a href="#entry-veda" class="skt-link">veda</a>, <a href="#entry-abdhi" class="skt-link">abdhi</a>, <a href="#entry-ambudhi" class="skt-link">ambudhi</a>, <a href="#entry-ko_na" class="skt-link">koṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-zruti" class="skt-link">śruti</a> ou <a href="#entry-ambhaa_msi" class="skt-link">ambhāṃsi</a> pour 4, <a href="#entry-indriya" class="skt-link">indriya</a>, <a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>, <a href="#entry-putra" class="skt-link">putra</a>, <a href="#entry-pavana" class="skt-link">pavana</a>, <a href="#entry-zara" class="skt-link">śara</a> ou <a href="#entry-suta_2" class="skt-link">suta_2</a> pour 5, <a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>, <a href="#entry-_rtu" class="skt-link">ṛtu</a> ou <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a> pour 6, <a href="#entry-sindhu" class="skt-link">sindhu</a>, <a href="#entry-adri" class="skt-link">adri</a>, <a href="#entry-parvata" class="skt-link">parvata</a>, <a href="#entry-mahiidhra" class="skt-link">mahīdhra</a> ou <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a> pour 7, <a href="#entry-anu_s_tubh_2" class="skt-link">anuṣṭubh_2</a>, <a href="#entry-kumbhin" class="skt-link">kumbhin</a>, <a href="#entry-gaja" class="skt-link">gaja</a>, <a href="#entry-giri" class="skt-link">giri</a> <a href="#entry-bhujafga" class="skt-link">bhujaṃga</a> ou <a href="#entry-vasu" class="skt-link">vasu</a> pour 8, <a href="#entry-chidra" class="skt-link">chidra</a> ou <a href="#entry-randhra" class="skt-link">randhra</a> pour 9, <a href="#entry-arka" class="skt-link">arka</a>, <a href="#entry-maasa" class="skt-link">māsa</a> ou <a href="#entry-ravi" class="skt-link">ravi</a> pour 12, <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a> pour 14, <a href="#entry-tithi" class="skt-link">tithi</a> pour 15, <a href="#entry-k_rti" class="skt-link">kṛti</a> pour 20, <a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a> pour 25, <a href="#entry-rada" class="skt-link">rada</a> ou <a href="#entry-danta" class="skt-link">danta</a> pour 32, <a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a> pour 33, etc.<br> <i>śarayamarasa</i> math. 625.<br> <i>śrutīndriyarasair</i> [stèle] en l'an 654 (de l'ère <a href="#entry-zaaka_3" class="skt-link">śāka_3</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutavidyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtavidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutavidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. le spiritisme ou science des esprits, un genre littéraire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutavij_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtavijñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-vij_naana" class="skt-link">vijñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutavij_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> spiritisme; démonologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutavikriyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtavikriyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vikriyaa" class="skt-link">vikriyā</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutavikriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> possession par des esprits malins; épilepsie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutayaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtayajña</i> [<a href="#entry-yaj_na" class="skt-link">yajña</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutayaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> [SB.] véd. le sacrifice aux esprits [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>], un des cinq rites majeurs [<a href="#entry-mahaayaj_na" class="skt-link">mahāyajña</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-bali" class="skt-link">bali</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuteza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūteśa</i> [<a href="#entry-iiza" class="skt-link">īśa</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuuteza" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhūteśa «Maître des esprits», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूति</span> <i>bhūti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> existence, bien-être <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bonheur, prospérité, fortune, réussite; salut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuutokti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūtokti</i> [<a href="#entry-ukti" class="skt-link">ukti</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuutokti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. parole divine infaillible.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूय</span> <i>bhūya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuuya" class="skt-link">n.</a> l'être, le devenir — ifc. fait de devenir ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> état de ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <i>kuśalaṃ</i> <i>bhūyāt</i> adr. souhait de prospérité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूयस्</span> <i>bhūyas</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-bhuuri" class="skt-link">bhūri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhuuyas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuuyas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuuyasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhūyasī</i> plus nombreux, plus abondant, plus grand <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui abonde en ⟨i. iic.⟩ — adv. plus, davantage, encore, beaucoup, très.<br> <i>bhūyasā</i> généralement; au plus haut degré.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>bhūyas</i> à quoi bon épiloguer?<br> <i>bhūyo'pi</i> de plus; encore plus.<br> <i>bhūyo</i> <i>bhūyas</i> encore et encore; bien des fois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuyastva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhūyastva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuuyastva" class="skt-link">n.</a> profusion, abondance.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuuyas" class="skt-link">bhūyas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuyazas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूयशस्</span> <i>bhūyaśas</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuuyas" class="skt-link">bhūyas</a><a href="#entry--zas" class="skt-link">-śas</a>] adv. principalement; généralement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> de nouveau; encore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuuyi_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भूयिष्ठ</span> <i>bhūyiṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-bhuuri" class="skt-link">bhūri</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhuuyi_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuuyi_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhuuyi_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhūyiṣṭhā</i> le plus grand, le plus abondant, principal — acc. <i>bhūyiṣṭham</i> adv. pour la plupart.<br> <i>kṣīṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ</i> <i>kṣapāyām</i> la nuit étant presque épuisée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुव</span> <i>bhuva</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuvas" class="skt-link">bhuvas</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhuva, épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> l'«Incréé» — <a href="#entry-bhuva" class="skt-link">n.</a> atmosphère — ifc. cf. <a href="#entry-bhu" class="skt-link">bhu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuvana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुवन</span> <i>bhuvana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuvana" class="skt-link">n.</a> être vivant, être humain, humanité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> univers, terre, un des 3 mondes [<a href="#entry-tribhuvana" class="skt-link">tribhuvana</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuvanadiipaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhuvanadīpaka</i> [<a href="#entry-diipaka" class="skt-link">dīpaka</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuvanadiipaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. du Bhuvanadīpaka, ouvrage de <a href="#entry-jyoti_sa" class="skt-link">jyotiṣa</a> de <a href="#entry-Upadmaprabha_1" class="skt-link">Padmaprabha_1</a> <a href="#entry-Usuuri" class="skt-link">Sūri</a>; <a href="#entry-Usi_mhatilaka" class="skt-link">Siṃhatilaka</a> <a href="#entry-Usuuri" class="skt-link">Sūri</a> en fit un commentaire [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] en 1269.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuvanatraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhuvanatraya</i> [<a href="#entry-traya" class="skt-link">traya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuvanatraya" class="skt-link">n.</a> les trois mondes, l'Univers; syn. <a href="#entry-tribhuvana" class="skt-link">tribhuvana</a>.<br> <i>svadeśo</i> <i>bhuvanatrayam</i> L'Univers est notre patrie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuvanavijaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhuvanavijaya</i> [<a href="#entry-vijaya" class="skt-link">vijaya</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuvanavijaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> hist. np. du Bhuvanavijaya, cour des poètes de l'empereur <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_nadeva" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇadeva</a> <a href="#entry-Uraaya" class="skt-link">Rāya</a> à <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-a_s_tadiggaja" class="skt-link">aṣṭadiggaja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuvanezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhuvaneśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-bhuvanezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Bhuvaneśvara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Seigneur de l'Univers» — <a href="#entry-bhuvanezvarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhuvaneśvarī</i> phil. [tantr.] np. de Bhuvaneśvarī, l'une des 10 puissances [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] de la Déesse [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de Bhuvaneśvarī_1, ville sacrée du <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a>-<a href="#entry-Uutkala" class="skt-link">Utkala</a> (mod. Bhubaneśvar, capitale de l'Orissa); cf. <a href="#entry-Ulifgaraaja" class="skt-link">Liṅgarāja</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuvana" class="skt-link">bhuvana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhuvas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुवस्</span> <i>bhuvas</i> [voc. pl. <a href="#entry-bhuu_2" class="skt-link">bhū_2</a>] ind. air, atmosphère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>bhuvar</i> [<i>bhuvarloka</i>] myth. np. de Bhuvar l'«Entre-Ciel-et-Terre», monde céleste [<a href="#entry-loka" class="skt-link">loka</a>] intermédiaire, où demeurent les accomplis [<a href="#entry-siddha_1" class="skt-link">siddha_1</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-lokatraya" class="skt-link">lokatraya</a>, <a href="#entry-vyaah_rti" class="skt-link">vyāhṛti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhu_njaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भुञ्जान</span> <i>bhuñjāna</i> [ppr. md. <a href="#entry-bhuj_2" class="skt-link">bhuj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-bhu_njaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-bhu_njaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-bhu_njaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>bhuñjānā</i> qui mange.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-bhvaadi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>bhvādi</i> [<a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">ādi</a>] <a href="#entry-bhvaadi" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. [«qui commence par <i>bhū</i>»] qualifie la première classe [<a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>] du présent des verbes.<br> <a href="#entry-bhuu_1" class="skt-link">bhū_1</a>
  </div>
</div>
